Neither the Embassy nor VFS asks for money beyond the published fees or holds a passport against your will, and the Embassy says such cases should be reported to the authorities <sup>[11](#o11)</sup>.

**Scams:** the Embassy and VFS take only the published fees, and paying an intermediary risks cancellation of the visa and legal action <sup>[11](#o11), [N9](#n9)</sup>. The Embassy has warned against brokers who sell a "Nulla Osta L/N code - carta blu": very few Bangladeshis with one got the visa, because most were not highly qualified, and a valid L/N nulla osta does not guarantee a visa <sup>[29](#o29)</sup>. Forging documents, signatures and seals is a crime <sup>[10](#o10)</sup>.

<a id="step-6-orientation-bmet-clearance-and-the-flight"></a>

### 3.6 Step 6: Orientation, BMET clearance and the flight

1. <a id="step-6-orientation-bmet-clearance-and-the-flight-item-1"></a>**Do the compulsory 3-day pre-departure orientation (PDO)** at one of BMET's 70 training institutes; the institute sets the fee, paid against a money receipt <sup>[4](#o4)</sup>. Institute list: [bmet.gov.bd](https://bmet.gov.bd/pages/static-pages/6922dd2c933eb65569e13cd6) <sup>[116](#o116)</sup>. Download your certificate at [training.oep.gov.bd/pdo-certificate](https://training.oep.gov.bd/pdo-certificate) with your passport or NID number and the captcha <sup>[117](#o117)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - How does an Italy-bound worker book a PDO place, and does any institute run an Italy session?

2. <a id="step-6-orientation-bmet-clearance-and-the-flight-item-2"></a>**Get BMET clearance (the smart card) yourself.** A worker who got the visa on their own applies at BMET's emigration branch in Dhaka or the Chattogram DEMO; it takes 3 working days <sup>[4](#o4)</sup>.

   You'll need <sup>[4](#o4)</sup>:

   - an application;
   - a copy of the visa;
   - the contract, if you have one;
   - an attested copy of your OEP registration;
   - an undertaking on Tk 300 non-judicial stamp paper;
   - the original pay order for the welfare fee and insurance.

   Your costs:

   - welfare fee to the Wage Earners' Welfare Board (the government's welfare board for migrant workers): Tk 5,500 <sup>[53](#o53)</sup>;
   - insurance: a one-time Tk 1,000 premium, 5 years' cover, Tk 10 lakh <sup>[54](#o54)</sup>;
   - clearance fee: Tk 250 <sup>[4](#o4)</sup>;
   - source tax on a single visa: Tk 400 (unattested visa) or Tk 250 (attested visa) <sup>[4](#o4)</sup>.

   A woman going on a single visa also needs a no-objection letter from her legal guardian on Tk 150 non-judicial stamp paper <sup>[118 (2021)](#o118)</sup>.

   **Check it is real** at [portal.wewb.gov.bd/bmet-wewbm-check](https://portal.wewb.gov.bd/bmet-wewbm-check) with your passport number and the captcha <sup>[119](#o119)</sup>; the airport welfare desks at Dhaka, Chattogram and Sylhet check every departing worker's smart card <sup>[4](#o4)</sup>. **If it fails:** BMET's Director (Emigration), +8802222222415, 01711-135901, diremi@bmet.gov.bd <sup>[4](#o4)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - Does BMET check an Italian nulla osta or visa before clearance, and how? BMET's online visa check covers only Saudi Arabia, Qatar, Bahrain, Singapore and the UAE <sup>[4](#o4)</sup>.
   > - Is an Italy worker always cleared as a "single visa", or must an agency file it?
   > - Is the source tax for an Italian visa Tk 400 or Tk 250 (does a stamped D visa count as "attested")?

3. <a id="step-6-orientation-bmet-clearance-and-the-flight-item-3"></a>**Before you fly,** keep one set of copies at home and at least 3 with you, open at least 2 bank accounts (one yours, one a family member's), and never carry a packet whose contents you don't know <sup>[54](#o54)</sup>. Carry the contacts of the Embassy of Bangladesh in Rome: Via dell'Antartide 5-7, 00144 Rome; +39 06 808 3595; emergency +39 3511 766 537 <sup>[120](#o120)</sup>. The contratto di soggiorno includes the employer's promise to pay your journey home ([Step 2, item 1: Get a named offer from the employer, not a "visa"](#step-2-the-job-offer-and-contract-item-1)) <sup>[55](#o55)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - Which airlines fly Dhaka to Rome or Milan in 2026, and what does a one-way ticket cost?

<a id="step-7-after-arrival"></a>

### 3.7 Step 7: After arrival

You have 8 working days from entry to apply for the permesso di soggiorno (residence permit), and 15 days to sign the contratto di soggiorno <sup>[9](#o9), [121](#o121), [8](#o8)</sup>.

1. <a id="step-7-after-arrival-item-1"></a>**Sign the contratto di soggiorno within 15 days of entering Italy.** You and the employer sign it, digitally or you by hand, and the employer uploads it to the SUI on the ALI portal within the same 15 days <sup>[8](#o8), [50](#o50), [21](#o21)</sup>. You may work on the nulla osta while it is being signed <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>. If it is not sent in time the nulla osta is revoked, unless the delay was not your fault <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - Do SUIs still call workers in person after arrival (for the integration agreement, the codice fiscale and the kit), or is everything now done online by the employer?

2. <a id="step-7-after-arrival-item-2"></a>**Get your codice fiscale (tax code).** You need the codice fiscale to enrol with the health service and choose a doctor <sup>[122 (2023)](#o122)</sup>. For a worker coming on a nulla osta, the SUI gives it <sup>[122 (2023)](#o122)</sup>.

   1. The SUI first gives a provisional code made only of numbers <sup>[122 (2023)](#o122)</sup>.
   2. The permanent code, of letters and numbers, is assigned when your permit is issued <sup>[122 (2023)](#o122)</sup>. It never expires <sup>[122 (2023)](#o122)</sup>.
   3. If your details produce the same code as someone else's, the SUI sends you to any Agenzia delle Entrate office for a permanent one <sup>[122 (2023)](#o122)</sup>.

   If you have no codice fiscale at all, go to any Agenzia delle Entrate office with form AA4/8, filled in and signed, and your passport with visa or your permit <sup>[122 (2023)](#o122)</sup>. The Agenzia publishes its guide to the codice fiscale for foreigners in Bengali <sup>[123](#o123)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - With only the provisional numeric code from the SUI, do banks, phone shops and the comune accept it, or do they ask for the permanent code?

3. <a id="step-7-after-arrival-item-3"></a>**Apply for the permesso di soggiorno through the SUI and the post office.** If you have a nulla osta, the SUI fills in the permit application and hands it to you in an envelope; you then take it to a post office with a Sportello Amico (the post-office counter that accepts permit applications) <sup>[88](#o88)</sup>. Some comuni (town councils) and authorised patronati (union-linked advice offices) also fill in the yellow-band kit free of charge <sup>[88](#o88)</sup>. Only you can hand it in, not a lawyer or agent <sup>[88](#o88)</sup>.

   You'll need <sup>[89](#o89), [124 (2024)](#o124), [88](#o88)</sup>:

   - the application from the SUI (or the yellow-band kit, one per person);
   - a copy of the nulla osta and of the signed contratto di soggiorno;
   - proof of where you live: a residence certificate, or a dichiarazione di ospitalità (the host's declaration that you live with them) with the rental contract and a copy of the host's ID;
   - a copy of your codice fiscale, if you already have it;
   - A4 copies of every passport page with your details, visas and stamps;
   - a copy of the UNILAV (the employer's hiring notice to the job centre);
   - the money below.

   **What it costs** <sup>[88](#o88), [121](#o121), [124 (2024)](#o124), [89](#o89), [1](#o1)</sup>:

   - EUR 16 (about Tk 2,230) revenue stamp (marca da bollo), bought at a tabaccheria (tobacconist);
   - EUR 30 (about Tk 4,190) paid to the post office clerk when you hand in the envelope;
   - one postal payment slip (bollettino) for EUR 30.46 (the permit card) plus a contribution: EUR 40 for a permit up to 1 year (slip total EUR 70.46, about Tk 9,840) or EUR 50 for a permit over 1 and up to 2 years (slip total EUR 80.46, about Tk 11,230).

   A permanent contract gives a permit of up to 2 years and a fixed-term one up to 1 year, so expect EUR 126.46 (about Tk 17,650) or EUR 116.46 (about Tk 16,260) in all <sup>[9](#o9), [88](#o88), [89](#o89), [1](#o1)</sup>.

   1. **Book a slot** at a Sportello Amico post office on poste.it or the Poste Italiane app (optional, saves queueing) <sup>[88](#o88)</sup>. The list of Sportello Amico offices is on poste.it, or call 800.200.309 free, Monday to Saturday, 8:00 to 20:00 <sup>[88](#o88)</sup>.
   2. **Fill in the bollettino** for the card plus contribution; slips are at every post office <sup>[88](#o88)</sup>.
   3. **Hand in the envelope open,** with your passport for identification and all copies <sup>[88](#o88)</sup>.
   4. **Keep the two papers the clerk gives you** <sup>[88](#o88)</sup>:
      - the ricevuta (receipt), with codes to follow your application on portaleimmigrazione.it;
      - the letter calling you to the Questura, with date, time and place; if no date can be set yet, the letter says it will come by registered post to the Italian address you gave <sup>[88](#o88)</sup>.
   5. **Go to the Questura on the date** for fotosegnalamento (photo and fingerprints) <sup>[121](#o121), [88](#o88)</sup>. Bring the original passport and originals of every document copied into the kit, the post-office receipt, the appointment letter and 4 passport-size photos <sup>[124 (2024)](#o124)</sup>.
   6. **Check when the card is ready** on poliziadistato.it after the fingerprint appointment <sup>[88](#o88)</sup>. The card shows your photo, fingerprints, codice fiscale and reason for stay <sup>[121](#o121)</sup>.

   **What the receipt allows:** with the receipt of a first work-permit application you have the same rights as a permit holder, you are lawfully present and you may work while you wait, unless the police notify you and your employer of a reason to refuse <sup>[121](#o121), [50](#o50)</sup>. Keep it with your passport: it lets you move around in Italy <sup>[88](#o88)</sup>.

   **How long it takes:** by law the permit is issued within 90 days <sup>[9](#o9)</sup>. In practice some Questure are far slower: in March 2024 Florence organisations reported first appointments being set for September 2025, and in February 2025 protesters there put the average wait to collect a permit at 1 year, 6 months and 15 days <sup>[N48 (2024)](#n48), [N49](#n49)</sup>. In January 2025 a man died of cold outside the Rome Questura's immigration office, where people queue overnight for appointments <sup>[N49](#n49)</sup>. Renewing the permit is in Your residence permit <sup>[9](#o9)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - How long after the post-office kit are Questura fingerprint appointments being set in Rome, Milan, Venice, Bologna and Naples in November 2026?
   > - How long after fingerprints does a first work permit actually arrive in those cities in 2026?
   > - Can a worker holding only the first-issue receipt fly to Bangladesh and back, and what do Questure tell Bangladeshi workers in 2026?

4. <a id="step-7-after-arrival-item-4"></a>**Sign the Accordo di integrazione (integration agreement).** Every newcomer over 16 asking for a permit of at least one year signs it with the state, at the SUI for a work permit; without it the permit is not issued <sup>[125](#o125), [121](#o121), [124 (2024)](#o124), [126](#o126)</sup>. You get a copy in a language you choose, or in English <sup>[127](#o127)</sup>.

   - **How it works:** you start with 16 credits and must reach 30 credits within two years, including spoken Italian at level A2 and basic knowledge of the Constitution and civil life (health, school, work, tax) <sup>[127](#o127), [128](#o128)</sup>. If you have children, you commit to send them to school <sup>[127](#o127)</sup>.
   - **The civic course:** a free session of 5 to 10 hours, within three months of signing; missing it costs 15 of your 16 credits <sup>[129](#o129)</sup>.
   - **Earning credits:** without certificates, the SUI can test you, also at a CPIA (public adult-education centre) <sup>[130](#o130)</sup>. Enrolling with the health service and choosing a doctor gives 4 credits ([Step 7, item 6: Enrol with the health service (SSN) and choose a doctor](#step-7-after-arrival-item-6)) <sup>[131 (2024)](#o131)</sup>.
   - **The check:** one month before the two years end, the SUI asks you to send documents within 15 days and offers a free test <sup>[128](#o128)</sup>. 30 credits or more closes the agreement; 1 to 29 extends it one year; zero or less means the permit is revoked or not renewed and you are expelled <sup>[128](#o128), [126](#o126)</sup>. Credits are also taken away for crimes or serious breaches of the law <sup>[132 (2024)](#o132)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - Do Prefetture in 2026 actually call Bangladeshi workers to the civic session, and is it in person or online?
   > - Has any Bangladeshi worker had a permit refused for failing the integration agreement check?

5. <a id="step-7-after-arrival-item-5"></a>**Register your residence at the comune (iscrizione anagrafica).** A non-EU citizen with a regular permit registers in the anagrafe (population register) of the comune where they live, on the same terms as Italians; in Rome it is compulsory <sup>[17](#o17), [133 (2024)](#o133)</sup>. The law requires you to ask for registration within 20 days of posting your permit application <sup>[134 (2024)](#o134)</sup>. You can register before the permit card arrives <sup>[135 (2024)](#o135), [136](#o136)</sup>.

   You'll need (Rome and Milan lists for a worker awaiting the first work permit) <sup>[135 (2024)](#o135), [136](#o136)</sup>:

   - a valid passport;
   - the post-office receipt of the permit application;
   - the SUI nulla osta, the contratto di soggiorno and the permit application filed at the SUI;
   - your codice fiscale (Milan asks for original and copy);
   - if others already live in the flat, a declaration from the resident accepting you, with a copy of their ID <sup>[135 (2024)](#o135)</sup>.

   1. **Rome:** fill in the ministerial residence form, sign it, and hand it to the anagrafe office of your Municipio (city district) in person, by PEC or ordinary email, or by registered letter, with a copy of your ID <sup>[135 (2024)](#o135)</sup>. It is free <sup>[135 (2024)](#o135)</sup>. The office registers you within two working days, and registration counts from the day you declared <sup>[133 (2024)](#o133)</sup>.
   2. **Milan:** apply online on the comune's form "Cambio di residenza per persone straniere provenienti dall'estero" and upload the documents; files sent by email are ignored, and each file may be up to 7MB <sup>[137](#o137), [138](#o138)</sup>. If you live as a guest, upload the host's consent declaration with the host's details and the rental or ownership references <sup>[138](#o138)</sup>.

   After every permit renewal, renew your declaration of residence at the anagrafe within 60 days, or you are removed from the register one year after the permit expires <sup>[135 (2024)](#o135)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - Do comuni with many Bangladeshis (Rome Municipio V and VI, Milan, Venice-Mestre, Monfalcone, Palermo) accept a residence at a shared flat where the landlord refuses to sign, and what do brokers charge for a residence address?

6. <a id="step-7-after-arrival-item-6"></a>**Enrol with the health service (SSN) and choose a doctor.** As an employed worker you must enrol in the SSN (Servizio Sanitario Nazionale, the national health service) and you get the same care as Italians; your dependent family members are covered too <sup>[139](#o139)</sup>. Go to the ASL (Azienda Sanitaria Locale, local health authority) of your comune of residence or, if you are not yet resident, of the address on your permit <sup>[131 (2024)](#o131), [139](#o139)</sup>.

   You'll need <sup>[140](#o140), [141](#o141), [122 (2023)](#o122)</sup>:

   - your permit or the post-office receipt of the application;
   - with only the receipt: your employment contract or last payslip;
   - your codice fiscale;
   - a signed undertaking to report any change in your status.

   1. **Choose your medico di base** (family doctor) at the ASL counter; this also earns 4 integration credits <sup>[131 (2024)](#o131)</sup>.
   2. **Collect the tessera sanitaria** (health card). With only the receipt, it is given on showing your passport and visa, and enrolment lasts up to 3 months, extendable <sup>[142 (2013)](#o142)</sup>. Enrolment ends when the permit expires or is revoked <sup>[141](#o141)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - In Rome and Milan in 2026, does the ASL enrol a newly arrived worker with only the post-office receipt and the provisional numeric codice fiscale?

7. <a id="step-7-after-arrival-item-7"></a>**Get an identity card (carta d'identità), if you want one.** Once registered as a resident, you can ask the comune for an electronic identity card (CIE); for non-EU residents it is valid only as ID in Italy, not for travel abroad <sup>[143](#o143)</sup>. In Rome, show a valid ID and your permit, book on prenotazionicie.interno.gov.it, and pay EUR 22.21 (about Tk 3,100); the card comes by post within 6 days <sup>[143](#o143), [144](#o144), [145](#o145), [1](#o1)</sup>.

8. <a id="step-7-after-arrival-item-8"></a>**SIM card and bank account.** To activate a SIM online, an operator such as ho. Mobile asks for your name, surname and codice fiscale and checks your ID document by video selfie <sup>[N50](#n50)</sup>. Opening a bank or post-office account is in Sending money home <sup>[N51 (2022)](#n51)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - Which operators' shops in Rome and Milan sell a SIM to a worker with only a passport and the provisional codice fiscale, and what do they charge?

<a id="scams"></a>

### 3.8 Scams

<a id="done-to-workers"></a>

#### 3.8.1 Done to workers

The main scams, each covered where it happens:

- **The fake or empty nulla osta.** Firms in Italy have filed hiring requests only to sell the nulla osta, with no job behind it, and networks have used employers' names without their knowledge ([Step 1, item 4: If a relative, broker or agency brings you an employer, check the employer can legally hire you](#step-1-find-a-real-job-item-4)) <sup>[N2](#n2), [N21](#n21), [N22](#n22)</sup>. In May 2025 the Embassy found fake or invalid nulla osta in over 70% of webform entries; check yours as in [Step 5: The visa at the Italian Embassy in Dhaka, and checking it is real](#step-5-the-visa-at-the-italian-embassy-in-dhaka-and-checking-it-is-real), "Check it is real" <sup>[11](#o11), [24](#o24)</sup>.
- **"I know someone at the Embassy."** Italian Foreign Ministry staff and two Bangladeshi middlemen were arrested in 2025 over exactly this offer (see [How a job reaches you](#how-a-job-reaches-you-and-who-takes-a-cut), and who takes a cut) <sup>[N25](#n25), [51](#o51)</sup>. The Embassy and VFS take only the published fees, and paying an intermediary risks cancellation of the visa and legal action <sup>[11](#o11), [N9](#n9)</sup>.
- **Paid appointments.** Embassy appointments are free, including through VFS; brokers have taken money promising to guarantee one ([Step 5, item 1: Register on VFS's work-visa webform](#step-5-the-visa-at-the-italian-embassy-in-dhaka-and-checking-it-is-real-item-1)) <sup>[101](#o101), [N20 (2024)](#n20)</sup>.
- **The "L/N carta blu" nulla osta,** sold as a sure route; it is only for highly qualified workers ([Step 5: The visa at the Italian Embassy in Dhaka, and checking it is real](#step-5-the-visa-at-the-italian-embassy-in-dhaka-and-checking-it-is-real), "Scams") <sup>[29](#o29)</sup>.
- **The passport held as security.** One broker kept a worker's passport until the last instalment; a ring working from a Gulshan-1 travel agency in Dhaka took a woman's passport and returned it only after she paid Tk 4.4 lakh into their bank accounts <sup>[N5](#n5), [N46](#n46)</sup>. Neither the Embassy nor VFS holds a passport against your will <sup>[11](#o11)</sup>.
- **Someone else flies on your visa.** In July 2026 the CID (Criminal Investigation Department) arrested seven members of the same ring, who had sent another woman to Italy on a woman's family visa; she learned at Dhaka airport that the other woman had flown on 24 April <sup>[N46](#n46)</sup>. The broker approached her as she collected her passport from the Embassy <sup>[N46](#n46)</sup>. Its alleged leader, Belayet Hossain, had sent about 30 people this way since 2016 <sup>[N46](#n46)</sup>.

Complain as in [Step 1, item 7: If a broker or agency takes your money](#step-1-find-a-real-job-item-7) <sup>[4](#o4)</sup>.

<a id="tried-by-workers"></a>

#### 3.8.2 Tried by workers

- **Fake Schengen visas.** In May 2026 three men flew to Rome on fake Schengen visas with a Biman ground officer's help; two were caught at Rome airport and sent back, after agreeing Tk 30 lakh each and paying Tk 20 lakh up front, and the CID arrested the officer <sup>[N44](#n44)</sup>. The Embassy warns that entering with a fraudulent visa leads to immediate repatriation <sup>[77](#o77)</sup>.
- **Two applications with two passports.** Only one visa application per person is allowed at a time <sup>[93](#o93), [105](#o105)</sup>.
- **Forged papers.** A nulla osta obtained with forged or fraudulent documents is refused or revoked, and forging or using a forged visa, permit or contratto di soggiorno carries 1 to 6 years' prison in Italy (see [What can get you jailed or deported](#what-can-get-you-jailed-or-deported)) <sup>[8](#o8), [9](#o9)</sup>. Italian embassies report every forged document behind a visa application to the Italian judicial authorities <sup>[113](#o113)</sup>.

<a id="your-rights-at-work"></a>

### 3.9 Your rights at work

With a valid permit you have, by law, the same treatment and rights at work as an Italian worker <sup>[68](#o68)</sup>. Minimum pay and overtime premiums are set by the CCNL (contratto collettivo nazionale di lavoro, the national collective contract) for your sector; if you came on a Decreto Flussi (Italy's yearly work-quota decree) nulla osta (work authorisation), a labour consultant or employer organisation had to certify that your employer follows it, as part of the employer's hiring request, unless you work as a domestic worker for an employer who is not self-sufficient (["Work visa: Decreto Flussi worker (non-seasonal)"](#work-visa-decreto-flussi-worker-non-seasonal), [Step 2: The job offer and contract](#step-2-the-job-offer-and-contract)) <sup>[67](#o67), [N28](#n28), [N29](#n29), [84](#o84), [8](#o8), [16](#o16)</sup>. Each CCNL has a code in the register of CNEL (Consiglio Nazionale dell'Economia e del Lavoro, the national council that keeps the archive of national collective contracts deposited with it); the main sector contracts, each signed by member federations of the trade union confederations CGIL, CISL and UIL, are A011 for farm workers, F012 for construction, H052 for hotels and tourism, H05Y for bars, restaurants and catering (the contract signed by the employers' federation FIPE), K511 for cleaning and multiservices, I100 for logistics and H501 for domestic work <sup>[146](#o146)</sup>. For unpaid or underpaid wages, overtime, 13th month or TFR (trattamento di fine rapporto, end-of-service pay), use the steps under If you aren't paid <sup>[147](#o147)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Which CCNL code do most Bangladeshi Decreto Flussi workers find on their contratto di soggiorno (contract of stay), and how often is it a "pirate" contract signed by minor unions with lower minimums instead of the CGIL-CISL-UIL contract for the sector?

<a id="pay-the-minimum-in-your-sector"></a>

#### 3.9.1 Pay: the minimum in your sector

Monthly or hourly minimums, gross (before tax and contributions), in the sectors where most Bangladeshis are hired, at EUR 1 = about Tk 139.6 <sup>[1](#o1)</sup>:

- **Farm work (operai agricoli):** the national floor for Area 3 (common workers) from 1 June 2026 is EUR 1,042.43 a month (about Tk 1,45,500), and for nursery and flower workers EUR 7.99 an hour (about Tk 1,115) <sup>[N28](#n28), [1](#o1)</sup>. Real farm pay is set in each province's contract, which may not go below these floors; provincial wages rose 3.4% on 1 June 2026 and rise another 1.7% on 1 January 2027 <sup>[N28](#n28)</sup>.
- **Construction (Edilizia Industria):** a common labourer (operaio comune) earns at least EUR 9.48 an hour (about Tk 1,323) nationally from 1 March 2026, the level I rate, or EUR 1,640.56 a month (about Tk 2,29,000), rising to EUR 9.77 an hour (about Tk 1,364) or EUR 1,690.56 a month (about Tk 2,36,000) on 1 March 2027 <sup>[N29](#n29), [N31](#n31), [1](#o1)</sup>. On top comes the indennità territoriale di settore (area allowance), which differs by province or region; in Ragusa, for example, a common labourer gets EUR 10.45 an hour or EUR 1,807.23 a month (about Tk 2,52,300) for 40 hours a week over 5 days <sup>[N31](#n31), [N52](#n52), [1](#o1)</sup>. Holiday pay and the Christmas bonus are set aside at 18.5% of gross pay and paid to you by the Cassa Edile (the construction industry's fund), not by the employer <sup>[N29](#n29)</sup>.
- **Hotels (CCNL Turismo):** from May 2026 at least EUR 1,412.60 a month at level 7 (about Tk 1,97,200) and EUR 1,507.45 at level 6 (EUR 8.76 an hour, about Tk 1,223), paid 14 times a year, with the 13th month in December and the 14th in July <sup>[N30](#n30), [1](#o1)</sup>. Seasonal hotel workers get the same minimums, and their 13th, 14th and unused leave are paid at the end of the job with the TFR (trattamento di fine rapporto, end-of-service pay; see End-of-service pay (TFR)) <sup>[N30](#n30)</sup>.
- **Bars, restaurants, pizzerias, canteens and catering:** a separate contract, the FIPE CCNL, not the hotel one <sup>[N30](#n30)</sup>.
- **Cleaning (pulizie e multiservizi):** a level-2 cleaner earns at least EUR 1,413.50 a month full-time from 1 October 2026 (about Tk 1,97,300), or EUR 8.17 an hour (about Tk 1,141); much cleaning work is part-time, and pay is cut in proportion to the hours <sup>[N32](#n32), [1](#o1)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - What are the 2026 FIPE minimums for a kitchen helper (aiuto cuoco) and a dishwasher (lavapiatti)?
> - What is the 2026 minimum for a warehouse or delivery worker under the logistics CCNL (Logistica, trasporto merci e spedizione)?
> - What daily wage (paga giornaliera) do fixed-term farm workers actually get in Latina, Caserta and Ragusa in 2026, against the provincial contract?

<a id="your-payslip-and-the-paga-globale-trick"></a>

#### 3.9.2 Your payslip, and the "paga globale" trick

Your employer must pay wages through a bank or post office (transfer, cheque or card), never in cash, and is fined EUR 1,000 to 5,000 (about Tk 1,39,600 to 6,98,000) for paying cash; opening an account is in Sending money home <sup>[57 (2018)](#o57), [1](#o1)</sup>. Your signature on the busta paga (payslip) does not prove you were paid, so keep your bank statements <sup>[57 (2018)](#o57)</sup>. The Labour Inspectorate takes claims for wages whether or not they appear on the payslip <sup>[147](#o147)</sup>.

A known trick in subcontracting is the "paga globale": a flat sum that folds in holiday pay, leave and TFR, while the payslip carries invented items that are never paid <sup>[N33 (2023)](#n33)</sup>. Among subcontractors at the Fincantieri shipyards, whose workers included Bangladeshis, the Guardia di Finanza (Italy's financial police) found 1,951 workers paid irregularly and about EUR 6 million (about Tk 84 crore) paid off the books; 383 workers took under EUR 7 gross an hour (about Tk 977) because they needed the job to renew their permit <sup>[N33 (2023)](#n33), [1](#o1)</sup>.

Keep your own sheet of the days and hours you work and every payment you receive; the Labour Inspectorate asks for exactly this ([If you aren't paid, item 2: File a request for inspection (Modulo INL 31) with the Ispettorato Territoriale del Lavoro (ITL, the Labour Inspectorate) of the province where you work](#if-you-arent-paid-item-2)) <sup>[147](#o147)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Does the "paga globale" still run among the Monfalcone shipyard subcontractors in 2026, and what hourly rate do Bangladeshi welders and fitters there actually take home?
> - Do employers make Decreto Flussi workers hand back part of their wage in cash each month (restituzione), and how much?

<a id="hours-rest-and-leave"></a>

#### 3.9.3 Hours, rest and leave

Normal hours are 40 a week <sup>[148](#o148)</sup>. With overtime, the average may not exceed 48 hours a week <sup>[149](#o149)</sup>. Overtime is counted separately and paid with the premium set in your CCNL; where the CCNL says nothing, it needs your agreement and is capped at 250 hours a year <sup>[67](#o67)</sup>. You are entitled to 11 hours' rest in a row every 24 hours, and at least 24 hours in a row every 7 days, normally on Sunday <sup>[150](#o150), [151](#o151)</sup>. Paid leave is at least four weeks a year, and it can't be swapped for money except when the job ends <sup>[152](#o152)</sup>.

<a id="sick-pay"></a>

#### 3.9.4 Sick pay

1. <a id="sick-pay-item-1"></a>**Go to your medico di base (family doctor),** chosen when you enrol with the health service (["Work visa: Decreto Flussi worker (non-seasonal)"](#work-visa-decreto-flussi-worker-non-seasonal), [Step 7: After arrival](#step-7-after-arrival)); the doctor sends the sickness certificate online to INPS (Istituto Nazionale della Previdenza Sociale, the national social security institute), so you don't send it yourself <sup>[131 (2024)](#o131), [153](#o153)</sup>.
2. <a id="sick-pay-item-2"></a>**Stay at home during check hours,** 10:00 to 12:00 and 17:00 to 19:00, every day on the certificate, weekends and holidays included <sup>[153](#o153)</sup>.

What you get: INPS pays from day 4, at 50% of average daily pay up to day 20 and 66.66% from day 21 to day 180; the first three days are paid by the employer only if your CCNL says so <sup>[153](#o153)</sup>.

<a id="injury-at-work"></a>

#### 3.9.5 Injury at work

Tell the employer at once, even about a small injury; if you don't and the employer doesn't know, the days before the employer learns of it are not paid <sup>[154](#o154)</sup>.

What you get: INAIL (Istituto Nazionale Assicurazione Infortuni sul Lavoro, the national work-accident insurer) pays 60% of daily pay from the fourth day after the accident while you can't work, and 75% from day 91 <sup>[155](#o155)</sup>. For long-term disability, death and an employer who hides the accident, see [If a worker dies](#if-a-worker-dies), is injured or loses the job <sup>[155](#o155)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Who issues the INAIL injury certificate to a worker (the hospital emergency department or the family doctor), and must the worker keep or send a copy?

<a id="end-of-service-pay-tfr"></a>

#### 3.9.6 End-of-service pay (TFR)

Every employee gets the TFR whenever the job ends, however it ends <sup>[156](#o156)</sup>. Each year adds that year's pay divided by 13.5, roughly one month's pay <sup>[156](#o156)</sup>. A missing TFR can be claimed through the Labour Inspectorate ([If you aren't paid, item 2: File a request for inspection (Modulo INL 31) with the Ispettorato Territoriale del Lavoro (ITL, the Labour Inspectorate) of the province where you work](#if-you-arent-paid-item-2)) <sup>[147](#o147)</sup>.

<a id="your-italian-pension"></a>

#### 3.9.7 Your Italian pension

The INPS contributions taken from your pay build an Italian pension you keep after going home for good; how to claim it from Bangladesh is under Coming home (see [Pension after you go home](#pension-after-you-go-home)) <sup>[157](#o157), [8](#o8)</sup>.

<a id="your-residence-permit"></a>

### 3.10 Your residence permit

<a id="how-long-you-can-stay"></a>

#### 3.10.1 How long you can stay

The permesso di soggiorno (residence permit) for work lasts as long as your contratto di soggiorno (contract of stay), up to 1 year on a fixed-term contract and 2 years on a permanent one <sup>[9](#o9)</sup>. Each renewal can last up to 3 years <sup>[9](#o9), [121](#o121)</sup>. While a permit is being issued, renewed or converted, the post-office receipt lets you stay and work, even after the 60-day limit, unless the police notify you of a reason to refuse <sup>[49](#o49)</sup>.

<a id="renewing"></a>

#### 3.10.2 Renewing

Ask the Questore (head of the Questura, the provincial police immigration office) of the province where you live to renew your permit at least 90 days before it expires <sup>[9](#o9)</sup>. If it expired more than 60 days ago and you did not ask to renew it, the Prefetto (the government's representative in the province) orders your expulsion <sup>[69](#o69)</sup>.

You'll need <sup>[88](#o88), [89](#o89)</sup>:

- the yellow-band kit (one per person), free at every post office <sup>[88](#o88)</sup>;
- your passport, and A4 copies of every passport page with your details, visas and stamps <sup>[88](#o88)</sup>;
- copies of your UNILAV (the employer's hiring notice to the job centre) and your last tax return or CUD (Certificazione Unica, the employer's yearly income statement) <sup>[89](#o89)</sup>;
- the money below.

**What it costs** <sup>[88](#o88), [121](#o121), [89](#o89), [1](#o1)</sup>:

- EUR 16 (about Tk 2,234) revenue stamp (marca da bollo), from a tabaccheria (tobacconist) <sup>[88](#o88), [1](#o1)</sup>;
- EUR 30 (about Tk 4,188) to the post-office clerk when you hand in the kit <sup>[88](#o88), [1](#o1)</sup>;
- one postal slip (bollettino): EUR 70.46 (about Tk 9,836) for a permit of up to 1 year, or EUR 80.46 (about Tk 11,232) for over 1 and up to 2 years <sup>[88](#o88), [121](#o121), [89](#o89), [1](#o1)</sup>.

1. <a id="renewing-item-1"></a>**Have the kit filled in.** Some comuni (town councils) and patronati fill it in free of charge <sup>[88](#o88)</sup>.
2. <a id="renewing-item-2"></a>**Hand it in, yourself, at a Sportello Amico post office** (the counters that take permit applications), open, with your passport and the copies; no agent or lawyer can do it for you <sup>[88](#o88)</sup>. Find the offices on poste.it or on 800.200.309, free, Monday to Saturday, 8:00 to 20:00 <sup>[88](#o88)</sup>.
3. <a id="renewing-item-3"></a>**Keep the receipt and the Questura appointment letter** the clerk gives you; the receipt lets you stay and work while you wait ([How long you can stay](#how-long-you-can-stay)) <sup>[88](#o88), [49](#o49)</sup>.
4. <a id="renewing-item-4"></a>**Go to the Questura on the date** to be fingerprinted again <sup>[9](#o9)</sup>. Then check when the card is ready on poliziadistato.it <sup>[88](#o88)</sup>.
5. <a id="renewing-item-5"></a>**Renew your residence registration at the comune within 60 days of every renewal,** or you are struck off the register one year after the permit expires <sup>[135 (2024)](#o135)</sup>.

What you get back: a permit, issued or renewed by law within 90 days <sup>[9](#o9)</sup>. A permit is also revoked or not renewed if your integration agreement (accordo di integrazione, signed on arrival) ends with zero credits (["Work visa: Decreto Flussi worker (non-seasonal)"](#work-visa-decreto-flussi-worker-non-seasonal), [Step 7: After arrival](#step-7-after-arrival)) <sup>[128](#o128)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - What contribution goes on the bollettino for a 3-year renewal (the 2017 decree lists EUR 40 and EUR 50 only for permits up to 2 years)?
> - How long do Questure in Rome, Milan, Venice and Naples take to renew a work permit in 2026, from kit to card?
> - Does the Questura renew the permit of a worker whose employer has stopped paying contributions, and on what proof?

<a id="if-you-lose-your-job"></a>

#### 3.10.3 If you lose your job

Losing the job, even by resigning, does not cancel your permit or your family's <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>. Register as available for work, and you may stay for the rest of the permit and at least one year (not on a seasonal permit), or as long as your unemployment pay lasts if that is longer; after that, a new permit depends on your income <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>. NASpI unemployment pay and how to claim it: If a worker dies, is injured or loses the job <sup>[65](#o65)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Where and how does a worker make the declaration of availability for work (dichiarazione di immediata disponibilità, DID): at the Centro per l'Impiego (public job centre), online, or through the NASpI claim?
> - How long does the Questura take to issue a permesso per attesa occupazione (job-seeking permit) in Rome and Milan in 2026, and what proof of the DID does it ask for?

<a id="long-term-eu-permit"></a>

#### 3.10.4 Long-term EU permit

After 5 years with a valid permit, you can ask the Questura for the EU long-term residence permit (permesso di soggiorno UE per soggiornanti di lungo periodo) <sup>[158](#o158)</sup>.

You'll need <sup>[158](#o158), [89](#o89)</sup>:

- lawful income of at least the yearly assegno sociale (the state's minimum social allowance), EUR 7,101.12 (about Tk 9,91,300) in 2026 <sup>[159](#o159), [1](#o1)</sup>;
- a pass in an Italian language test <sup>[158](#o158)</sup>;
- a housing suitability certificate from your comune (how to get one: Family reunification, [Step 2: Apply for the nulla osta on the ALI portal](#step-2-apply-for-the-nulla-osta-on-the-ali-portal)) <sup>[89](#o89)</sup>;
- the post-office kit, with a slip of EUR 130.46 (about Tk 18,212), which includes a EUR 100 contribution <sup>[89](#o89), [121](#o121), [1](#o1)</sup>.

What you get back: a permit issued within 90 days, valid 10 years and renewed automatically <sup>[158](#o158)</sup>. Your children under 18 living with you get the long-term permit too, you no longer have to prove Italian at B1 for citizenship, and losing all integration-agreement credits no longer costs you the permit <sup>[160](#o160), [161](#o161), [126](#o126)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - What level is the Italian test for the long-term permit, where is it booked, and does it cost anything?

<a id="becoming-an-italian-citizen"></a>

#### 3.10.5 Becoming an Italian citizen

You can be granted Italian citizenship after ten years' uninterrupted legal residence; a June 2025 referendum to cut this to five years failed for lack of quorum <sup>[162 (2024)](#o162), [161](#o161), [N53](#n53)</sup>.

**Who qualifies:**

- **Ten years' legal residence:** registered as a resident at the comune and in order with permit rules for the whole period <sup>[161](#o161)</sup>.
- **Income:** enough to support yourself <sup>[163](#o163)</sup>.
- **No criminal record** and no security bar <sup>[163](#o163)</sup>.
- **Italian at B1 or above,** proved by an Italian school diploma, a certificate from a recognised body, or an attestation from a CPIA (Centro Provinciale per l'Istruzione degli Adulti, the public adult school); EU long-term permit holders are exempt <sup>[164](#o164), [161](#o161)</sup>.

1. <a id="becoming-an-italian-citizen-item-1"></a>**Get your Bangladeshi birth certificate and police clearance;** the police clearance is valid six months from issue, the birth certificate does not expire <sup>[161](#o161)</sup>.
2. <a id="becoming-an-italian-citizen-item-2"></a>**Make every name match** across your Italian and Bangladeshi documents; any difference needs an attestation from the Bangladesh Embassy, legalised by the Prefettura (the central government's office in the province) <sup>[161](#o161)</sup>.
3. <a id="becoming-an-italian-citizen-item-3"></a>**Pay EUR 250** (about Tk 34,900) by post-office bollettino, bank transfer or PagoPA (Italy's online payment system for public bodies) <sup>[165](#o165), [161](#o161), [1](#o1)</sup>.
4. <a id="becoming-an-italian-citizen-item-4"></a>**Apply online** at portaleservizi.dlci.interno.it <sup>[161](#o161)</sup>.
5. <a id="becoming-an-italian-citizen-item-5"></a>**Wait:** the law gives the Ministry 24 months to decide, extendable to 36 <sup>[166](#o166)</sup>.
6. <a id="becoming-an-italian-citizen-item-6"></a>**Read the decision** on SEND, the state's digital notifications platform, which also tells you how to take the oath <sup>[161](#o161)</sup>.

Whether you keep Bangladeshi citizenship depends on Bangladesh's law <sup>[161](#o161)</sup>. Children: see Having a child in Italy <sup>[167](#o167)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - What income does the Ministry expect in 2026 for a single applicant and for a family (older circulars used EUR 8,263.31 and EUR 11,362.05)?
> - Does Bangladesh let a Bangladeshi who becomes Italian keep Bangladeshi citizenship, and how is it recorded at the Embassy in Rome?
> - How long are Bangladeshi citizenship applications taking in 2026?

<a id="what-can-get-you-jailed-or-deported"></a>

### 3.11 What can get you jailed or deported

<a id="no-valid-permit"></a>

#### 3.11.1 No valid permit

Entering or staying in Italy without a valid permit is a crime, fined EUR 5,000 to 10,000 (about Tk 6.98 lakh to 13.96 lakh) <sup>[70](#o70), [1](#o1)</sup>. A permit that expired more than 60 days ago without a renewal request leads to expulsion ([Renewing](#renewing)) <sup>[69](#o69)</sup>. If the police ask, you must show your passport and permit; refusing without good reason can mean arrest of up to a year and a fine of up to EUR 2,000 (about Tk 2.79 lakh) <sup>[17](#o17), [1](#o1)</sup>.

<a id="expulsion-and-cpr-detention"></a>

#### 3.11.2 Expulsion and CPR detention

You can ask for 7 to 30 days to leave by yourself, including through an assisted voluntary return programme ([Going home without valid papers](#going-home-without-valid-papers)) <sup>[69](#o69)</sup>. If you can't be removed at once, the Questore can hold you in a CPR (Centro di Permanenza per i Rimpatri, repatriation detention centre): three months at first, extendable by three months and then by up to twelve more months in total <sup>[76](#o76)</sup>. At the court hearing that confirms your removal you have the right to free legal aid and an interpreter <sup>[69](#o69)</sup>. An expulsion brings a 3 to 5 year entry ban, recorded in the Schengen Information System, which closes every EU and Schengen country, not only Italy; coming back without authorisation is punished by 1 to 4 years in prison <sup>[69](#o69)</sup>. Where irregular stays end, and the CPRs: Irregular routes and undocumented workers <sup>[76](#o76)</sup>.

**Help from the Bangladesh side:** the Consulate General in Milan visits Bangladeshis held in Italian jails, and gives a certificate of financial hardship so a person who can't pay gets a free lawyer from the Italian state ([Help from the Bangladesh Embassy in Rome and Consulate in Milan](#help-from-the-bangladesh-embassy-in-rome-and-consulate-in-milan)) <sup>[168 (2022)](#o168), [169 (2022)](#o169)</sup>.

<a id="fake-documents"></a>

#### 3.11.3 Fake documents

Forging a visa, permit or contratto di soggiorno, using one, or forging papers to obtain them, is punished by 1 to 6 years in prison <sup>[9](#o9)</sup>. How fake papers are sold: Work visa: Decreto Flussi worker (non-seasonal), Scams <sup>[9](#o9)</sup>.

<a id="drugs-and-crime"></a>

#### 3.11.4 Drugs and crime

Selling drugs, or holding them to sell, is punished by 6 to 20 years and a fine of EUR 26,000 to 260,000 (about Tk 36.3 lakh to 3.63 crore), or 6 months to 5 years for minor cases <sup>[170](#o170), [1](#o1)</sup>. Even possession for your own use brings administrative penalties, including suspension of your passport <sup>[171](#o171)</sup>. A conviction for a serious crime, even before appeal, bars a permit <sup>[172](#o172)</sup>. A judge can replace a prison sentence of up to two years with expulsion <sup>[173](#o173)</sup>.

<a id="working-without-papers"></a>

#### 3.11.5 Working without papers

An employer who employs someone without a valid permit faces 6 months to 3 years in prison and a EUR 5,000 (about Tk 6.98 lakh) fine per worker <sup>[8](#o8), [1](#o1)</sup>. If you are exploited by a caporale (an illegal gangmaster), a special permit protects you when you report it ([Caporalato: illegal gangmasters](#caporalato-illegal-gangmasters)) <sup>[174](#o174)</sup>.

<a id="if-you-arent-paid"></a>

### 3.12 If you aren't paid

**If you are not paid, paid less than your CCNL, or your overtime, 13th month or TFR is missing, act in this order:**

1. <a id="if-you-arent-paid-item-1"></a>**Write to the employer by registered letter with return receipt (raccomandata A/R)** asking for the money; this formal notice (messa in mora) stops your claim from expiring <sup>[147](#o147)</sup>.
   Sample wording: "Io sottoscritto [name], nato a [place] il [date], codice fiscale [number], dipendente dal [start date] con mansione di [job], chiedo il pagamento delle retribuzioni non pagate per i mesi di [months], pari a euro [amount], oltre a [straordinari / tredicesima / TFR]. In mancanza, mi rivolgerò all'Ispettorato del Lavoro e al giudice." ("I, [name], born in [place] on [date], tax code [number], employed since [start date] as [job], ask for payment of unpaid wages for [months], EUR [amount], plus [overtime / 13th month / TFR]. Otherwise I will go to the Labour Inspectorate and the court.") <sup>[147](#o147)</sup>
2. <a id="if-you-arent-paid-item-2"></a>**File a request for inspection (Modulo INL 31) with the Ispettorato Territoriale del Lavoro (ITL, the Labour Inspectorate) of the province where you work** <sup>[147](#o147)</sup>.
   You'll need <sup>[147](#o147)</sup>:
   - a copy of your passport or permit (without it the complaint is not accepted) <sup>[147](#o147)</sup>;
   - copies of payslips, bank statements or receipts, and your own sheet of days and hours worked <sup>[147](#o147)</sup>.
   1. Download Modulo INL 31 from ispettorato.gov.it; it exists in English and in Bangla <sup>[175](#o175)</sup>.
   2. Tick what is owed: unpaid wages (on or off the payslip), the 13th or 14th month, TFR, unpaid overtime, or work "in nero" (off the books) <sup>[147](#o147)</sup>.
   3. Write the facts in Bangla if you need to, but add Italian too <sup>[147](#o147)</sup>.
   4. Send it to the ITL (Ispettorato territoriale del lavoro, the local labour inspectorate) of the province where you work; the list of offices is at ispettorato.gov.it/contatti <sup>[147](#o147)</sup>.

   What happens: the ITL may call you and the employer to a conciliation, where a union or a professional can assist you <sup>[147](#o147), [176](#o176)</sup>. If inspectors find money owed, they order the employer to pay (diffida accertativa), and the employer has 30 days to seek conciliation <sup>[177](#o177)</sup>. Form 31 can't be used against a dismissal ([Changing employer](#changing-employer)) <sup>[147](#o147)</sup>.
3. <a id="if-you-arent-paid-item-3"></a>**Go to a union or a patronato,** which serve workers with a permit, can assist you at the conciliation, and can send resignation forms and NASpI claims for you <sup>[8](#o8), [176](#o176), [64](#o64), [65](#o65)</sup>.
4. <a id="if-you-arent-paid-item-4"></a>**Tell the Bangladesh Embassy's labour welfare wing** in Rome, welfare.rome@gmail.com, which visits workplaces to check conditions and pay; Milan's is fslbcgmilan@gmail.com <sup>[178 (2021)](#o178), [179](#o179)</sup>.

If the pay is far below the CCNL and comes with degrading work or housing, it may be caporalato (illegal gangmaster labour), a crime: see Caporalato: illegal gangmasters <sup>[92 (2016)](#o92), [174](#o174)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Which CGIL, CISL or UIL union offices in Rome, Milan, Venice-Mestre, Naples and Latina have Bangla-speaking staff, and what are their phone numbers?
> - How long does an ITL in Rome or Venice take, in practice, from Form 31 to a diffida accertativa?
> - Do workers who complain lose the employer's help with the permit renewal, and does the Questura accept a renewal backed by a pending labour case?
> - Does the Rome Embassy's labour wing act on wage complaints against Italian employers, and how?

<a id="changing-employer-or-leaving"></a>

### 3.13 Changing employer or leaving

<a id="changing-employer"></a>

#### 3.13.1 Changing employer

A permit for employment can be used for other legal work, and losing or leaving a job does not cancel it ([If you lose your job](#if-you-lose-your-job)) <sup>[17](#o17), [8](#o8)</sup>. The exceptions are EU Blue Card holders (the permit for highly qualified workers) and out-of-quota care workers, who need the labour office's prior approval to change employer in their first 12 months <sup>[66](#o66), [27](#o27)</sup>. In August 2026 Bangladesh raised with Italy the case of several hundred workers who entered regularly in 2022-23 but cannot change employer or extend their permits because their sponsors can't be reached <sup>[N36](#n36)</sup>. If the Decreto Flussi employer loses interest after you arrive and never signs the contratto di soggiorno, a 2007 circular allows a permesso per attesa occupazione (job-seeking permit), but only with the written authorisation of the head of the SUI (Sportello Unico per l'Immigrazione, the immigration desk at the Prefettura); Questure issued 886 job-seeking permits in all in 2023, 835 in 2024 and 666 in 2025 <sup>[N11](#n11)</sup>. In one case caseworkers followed, no other employer could sign the contract, because only the employer who had called the worker could hire her for the permit <sup>[N11](#n11)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - How does a worker ask the head of the SUI for a job-seeking permit when the Decreto Flussi employer never signs, how long does it take, and have TAR rulings (TAR Veneto, TAR Campania, 2024-2025) let another employer take the worker on?
> - What does a new employer have to file, and where, to take on a worker whose permit names another employer, and does the Questura accept the new employer at the first renewal?

**If you resign:** resign only online, on the Ministry of Labour form at lavoro.gov.it; a paper letter has no effect <sup>[64](#o64)</sup>. A patronato, union or the ITL can send it for you, and you can withdraw it within seven days <sup>[64](#o64)</sup>. Resigning loses NASpI unemployment pay, unless you resign for just cause, forced by the employer's conduct so the job cannot go on <sup>[65](#o65)</sup>.

**If you are dismissed:** challenge the dismissal in writing within 60 days of receiving it (a union letter counts), then file at the labour court (giudice del lavoro) or ask for conciliation within 180 days, or you lose the right <sup>[180](#o180)</sup>.

<a id="going-home-on-leave"></a>

#### 3.13.2 Going home on leave

**With only the renewal receipt** (permit expired, renewal filed at the post office), the Polizia di Stato lets you leave and come back showing your valid passport, the post-office receipt and the expired permit, original or copy <sup>[181 (2007)](#o181)</sup>. If you are waiting for your first permit, you must leave and come back through the same border crossing, with no transit through another Schengen country, and the border officer stamps both the passport and the receipt <sup>[182 (2008)](#o182)</sup>.

**If you are stuck in Bangladesh** (permit expired, lost or stolen), apply for a re-entry visa (visto di reingresso) at the Italian Embassy in Dhaka; it is issued only after the Questura in Italy clears it <sup>[183](#o183)</sup>.

You'll need <sup>[183](#o183)</sup>:

- the national (type D) visa form and one passport-standard photo to ICAO (International Civil Aviation Organization) rules (3.5 x 4 cm, white background, taken in the last 6 months) <sup>[183](#o183)</sup>;
- a passport valid at least 15 more months, with two blank visa pages <sup>[183](#o183)</sup>;
- if the permit expired no more than 60 days before you apply: a copy of the expired permit and the renewal receipt <sup>[183](#o183)</sup>;
- if it was lost or stolen: an apostilled police report from the country where it happened <sup>[183](#o183)</sup>;
- proof of the date you left Italy (the exit stamp, or the ticket if the passport was lost) <sup>[183](#o183)</sup>;
- a letter in English or Italian explaining why you need to return <sup>[183](#o183)</sup>;
- a flight booking <sup>[183](#o183)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Is the 2007-2008 Polizia di Stato guidance on travelling with the receipt still applied in 2026, and do Rome Fiumicino and Milan Malpensa let workers fly via the Gulf or Istanbul (Emirates, Qatar, Turkish) with only the receipt?
> - How long does the Dhaka re-entry visa take in 2026, and what happens if the permit expired more than 60 days before?

<a id="leaving-at-the-end-of-the-job"></a>

#### 3.13.3 Leaving at the end of the job

1. <a id="leaving-at-the-end-of-the-job-item-1"></a>**Check your last payslip** for unused leave, the 13th (and 14th) month pro rata, and the TFR; claim anything missing through If you aren't paid <sup>[152](#o152), [N30](#n30), [156](#o156)</sup>.
2. <a id="leaving-at-the-end-of-the-job-item-2"></a>**Claim NASpI within 68 days** if the job ended against your will ([If a worker dies](#if-a-worker-dies), is injured or loses the job) <sup>[65](#o65)</sup>.
3. <a id="leaving-at-the-end-of-the-job-item-3"></a>**If you leave Italy for good,** your INPS contributions stay yours for a pension ([Your Italian pension](#your-italian-pension)) <sup>[157](#o157)</sup>.

<a id="bringing-your-family"></a>

### 3.14 Bringing your family

You can bring your wife and children under 18 once you hold a permit of at least one year, earn above the income line and have a home the comune certifies as suitable; for your wife (not your children) you must also have lived legally in Italy for two years without a break <sup>[184](#o184), [71](#o71)</sup>. With a contract of at least one year, your family can instead come with you on your first entry, if you meet the same housing and income conditions <sup>[71](#o71)</sup>. Blue Card holders can bring family whatever the length of their permit <sup>[66](#o66)</sup>. The whole procedure, from the online application to the visa in Dhaka, is in Family reunification <sup>[71](#o71), [185](#o185)</sup>. Your wife can work in Italy once she arrives on her family permit <sup>[186](#o186), [30](#o30)</sup>.

<a id="work-visa-seasonal-worker"></a>

## 4 Work visa: seasonal worker

A seasonal permit lets you work in Italy on farms or in tourism and hotels for at most nine months in any twelve, then go home <sup>[26](#o26)</sup>. Many in Bangladesh call it the কৃষি ভিসা (agriculture visa) <sup>[N3](#n3)</sup>. Bangladesh is on the list of countries whose citizens can take these jobs, and you can't apply yourself: an employer in Italy, or its trade association, files a named request for you <sup>[187](#o187), [26](#o26)</sup>.

For Bangladeshis the nulla osta (the work authorisation issued by the SUI, the Sportello Unico per l'Immigrazione, the immigration desk at each province's Prefettura, the state's office in the province) is never issued by silent consent: the Questura (provincial police headquarters) must approve it, and the INL (Ispettorato Nazionale del Lavoro, the national labour inspectorate) first checks the employer, together with AGEA (the state agency for farm payments) for farm jobs <sup>[16](#o16)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - How many seasonal nulla osta were issued to Bangladeshis in 2025 and 2026, and how many led to a visa at the Italian Embassy in Dhaka?
> - Does the Embassy in Dhaka process seasonal work visas in 2026, and how long does a seasonal applicant wait for an appointment?

<a id="how-the-seasonal-quota-works"></a>

### 4.1 How the seasonal quota works

Italy allows 88,000 seasonal entries in 2026, 89,000 in 2027 and 90,000 in 2028, for all countries together <sup>[187](#o187), [6](#o6)</sup>. Most places are reserved for applications filed through employers' organisations, so who files decides whether you can be brought in <sup>[187](#o187), [188](#o188)</sup>:

- **Farm jobs through farmers' organisations:** 47,000 places a year, for applications filed by Coldiretti (the national confederation of direct farmers), CIA (the Italian farmers' confederation), Confagricoltura (the general confederation of Italian agriculture) and the farm cooperative federations <sup>[187](#o187), [188](#o188)</sup>.
- **Tourism jobs through tourism employers' organisations:** 13,000 places in 2026 and 14,000 in 2027 <sup>[187](#o187)</sup>.
- **Returning seasonal workers:** 5,000 places in 2026 for workers who have done seasonal work in Italy at least once in the last five years and whose employer asks for a multi-year nulla osta <sup>[188](#o188)</sup>.

A private employer filing alone may send at most three nulla osta requests a year; the limit does not apply to employers' organisations, labour consultants and staffing agencies <sup>[16](#o16), [49](#o49)</sup>.

**Dates for the 2027 round:** employers pre-fill applications on the ALI portal (the Interior Ministry's website for nulla osta requests) from 9:00 on 23 October 2026 to 20:00 on 7 December 2026 <sup>[6](#o6), [49](#o49)</sup>. The click day (the moment the online quota opens) is 12 January 2027 from 9:00 for farm jobs (form C-Stag agricolo) and 9 February 2027 for tourism jobs (form C-Stag turistico); applications are handled mostly in order of arrival <sup>[6](#o6), [50](#o50)</sup>. Applications stay open until 31 December 2027 while places remain <sup>[6](#o6)</sup>. A 2026 application that had not won a place by 30 June 2027, or a 2026 nulla osta not followed by a visa by then, lapses <sup>[16](#o16)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - How many of the 2026 farm and tourism places went to applications for Bangladeshi workers?
> - Which Coldiretti, CIA or Confagricoltura provincial offices filed applications for Bangladeshi workers in 2026?

<a id="step-1-find-a-real-seasonal-employer"></a>

### 4.2 Step 1: Find a real seasonal employer

1. <a id="step-1-find-a-real-seasonal-employer-item-1"></a>**Know who can hire you.** Only an employer in Italy, or its trade association on its behalf, can ask for you by name <sup>[26](#o26)</sup>. The nulla osta is refused or revoked if the employer has been sanctioned for undeclared work or has no real business <sup>[26](#o26)</sup>. An employer that did not sign the contratto di soggiorno (the contract of stay signed after you arrive) for workers it asked for in the last three years cannot apply, nor can one sent to trial for slavery, trafficking or caporalato (illegal gangmaster labour) <sup>[16](#o16)</sup>. How to check an employer: see ["Work visa: Decreto Flussi worker (non-seasonal)"](#work-visa-decreto-flussi-worker-non-seasonal), [Step 1: Find a real job](#step-1-find-a-real-job).

   **Scams:** in Bangladesh, one man paid an agency over EUR 7,000 (about Tk 9.8 lakh) for an employer contact and expected to pay as much again when the visa came <sup>[N4](#n4), [1](#o1)</sup>. Networks have filed applications in the names of employers who did not know their names were used <sup>[N22](#n22)</sup>. One, broken up in Grosseto in September 2025, brought in nearly 200 Bangladeshis with fake employers, charging EUR 2,000-4,000 (about Tk 2.8-5.6 lakh) per application, collected in Bangladesh and Italy; some families mortgaged land to pay <sup>[N22](#n22), [1](#o1)</sup>. In a network broken up around Latina in 2014, about 40 farm employers had filed false hiring requests for money, and migrants paid the network EUR 6,000-8,000 (about Tk 8.4-11.2 lakh) each <sup>[189 (2014)](#o189), [1](#o1)</sup>. One worker who arrived for a company that did not exist fell into irregular status <sup>[N4](#n4)</sup>. More: Work visa: Decreto Flussi worker (non-seasonal), Scams.

   > **Need to know**
   > - What does a seasonal farm nulla osta sell for in Dhaka, Cumilla, Shariatpur and Madaripur in 2026?
   > - Can a worker in Bangladesh ask Coldiretti, CIA or Confagricoltura to confirm that they filed an application in the worker's name, and how?
   > - Do Bangladeshi-owned businesses in Italy (shops, restaurants) file seasonal applications for relatives, and in which provinces?

2. <a id="step-1-find-a-real-seasonal-employer-item-2"></a>**If you have worked a season in Italy before, ask the same employer to call you back.** A seasonal worker admitted at least once in the last five years, who kept to the permit's conditions and went home when it expired, has priority over first-timers, with the same or another employer <sup>[16](#o16)</sup>. The employer can ask for a three-year nulla osta for you (see [Staying on: another season or a non-seasonal permit](#staying-on-another-season-or-a-non-seasonal-permit)) <sup>[188](#o188), [26](#o26)</sup>.

<a id="step-2-the-employer-applies-on-click-day"></a>

### 4.3 Step 2: The employer applies on click day

The employer, or its trade association, does this step; you can only check it was done <sup>[26](#o26)</sup>.

1. <a id="step-2-the-employer-applies-on-click-day-item-1"></a>**The employer pre-fills and sends form C-Stag on the ALI portal,** C-Stag agricolo for farm jobs and C-Stag turistico for tourism, from 9:00 on click day, after pre-filling it <sup>[6](#o6), [49](#o49)</sup>. Seasonal jobs skip the check at the Centro per l'Impiego (public job centre) that non-seasonal and domestic jobs need <sup>[16](#o16)</sup>. The portal and the employer's documents: see ["Work visa: Decreto Flussi worker (non-seasonal)"](#work-visa-decreto-flussi-worker-non-seasonal), [Step 3: The employer gets your nulla osta](#step-3-the-employer-gets-your-nulla-osta).
2. <a id="step-2-the-employer-applies-on-click-day-item-2"></a>**The SUI issues the nulla osta** within 20 days of the application winning a quota place <sup>[16](#o16)</sup>. For a Bangladeshi it first waits for the Questura's approval and the INL's check of the employer <sup>[16](#o16)</sup>.

**Check it is real:** as for any nulla osta (see ["Work visa: Decreto Flussi worker (non-seasonal)"](#work-visa-decreto-flussi-worker-non-seasonal), [Step 5: The visa at the Italian Embassy in Dhaka, and checking it is real](#step-5-the-visa-at-the-italian-embassy-in-dhaka-and-checking-it-is-real)).

> **Need to know**
> - How long do Questura and INL checks on seasonal nulla osta for Bangladeshis actually take in 2026?

<a id="step-3-visa-documents-and-the-flight"></a>

### 4.4 Step 3: Visa, documents and the flight

You apply at VFS Global, the company that takes all Italian visa applications in Bangladesh, and give fingerprints, as every work visa applicant must <sup>[10](#o10), [2](#o2)</sup>. Seasonal visas for workers from Bangladesh and the other countries Italy treats as "at risk" (Pakistan, Sri Lanka and Morocco) have been issued about ten months late <sup>[N11](#n11)</sup>. The fees, documents and Bangladesh-side steps are the same as for the non-seasonal worker (see ["Work visa: Decreto Flussi worker (non-seasonal)"](#work-visa-decreto-flussi-worker-non-seasonal), [Step 4: Passport, police clearance and certificates](#step-4-passport-police-clearance-and-certificates); [Step 5: The visa at the Italian Embassy in Dhaka, and checking it is real](#step-5-the-visa-at-the-italian-embassy-in-dhaka-and-checking-it-is-real); [Step 6: Orientation, BMET clearance and the flight](#step-6-orientation-bmet-clearance-and-the-flight)).

<a id="step-4-after-arrival"></a>

### 4.5 Step 4: After arrival

1. <a id="step-4-after-arrival-item-1"></a>**Start work, and sign the contratto di soggiorno.** You can start work as soon as you arrive with the nulla osta and visa, before the contract is signed <sup>[16](#o16)</sup>. Signing, the permesso di soggiorno (residence permit) and the codice fiscale (tax code): see ["Work visa: Decreto Flussi worker (non-seasonal)"](#work-visa-decreto-flussi-worker-non-seasonal), [Step 7: After arrival](#step-7-after-arrival). In practice, workers have waited up to a year for a Prefettura appointment to sign, and employers often disappear <sup>[N6](#n6)</sup>. One Bangladeshi farm worker who arrived in October 2022 was paid and housed, never got the appointment, and three years later had his nulla osta revoked <sup>[N6](#n6)</sup>. Another, who entered legally in 2023, was refused a permit by the Latina Questura, ordered expelled and held at Ponte Galeria in Rome, a CPR (Centro di permanenza per il rimpatrio, a detention centre for people being deported) <sup>[N6](#n6)</sup>. Bangladeshis who entered on the Decreto Flussi and found no job, because the employer could not be found or was a scam, have formed a committee in Rome, Tikase <sup>[N6](#n6)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - Now that the contract is uploaded online within 15 days of entry, do seasonal workers still wait for a Prefettura appointment, and in which provinces (Rome, Latina, Naples, Caserta)?

2. <a id="step-4-after-arrival-item-2"></a>**Find your SIISL account.** Once the contract is signed, INPS (Istituto Nazionale della Previdenza Sociale, the national social security institute) enrols you in SIISL (the Information System for Social and Labour Inclusion, the government's job-matching platform) <sup>[26](#o26)</sup>. Through SIISL you can take a seasonal job with another employer while your nulla osta is valid, without going home for a new visa <sup>[26](#o26)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - How does a seasonal worker log in to SIISL without SPID (Italy's digital identity)?

<a id="your-rights-as-a-seasonal-worker"></a>

### 4.6 Your rights as a seasonal worker

Farm and hotel pay follow their own national collective contracts (CCNL, contratto collettivo nazionale di lavoro) <sup>[N28](#n28), [N30](#n30)</sup> (see [Your rights at work](#your-rights-at-work)). What is different for seasonal workers:

- **Hotel pay:** seasonal hotel workers get the same minimums as permanent staff; their 13th and 14th month's pay and unused leave build up month by month and are paid at the end with the TFR (trattamento di fine rapporto, end-of-service pay) <sup>[N30](#n30)</sup>.
- **Housing:** if the employer gives you a place to live, the rent can't be more than one third of your pay and can't be taken automatically from your wages <sup>[26](#o26)</sup>. An employer who gives unfit housing, overcharges or deducts rent from pay is fined EUR 350-5,500 (about Tk 49,000-7.7 lakh) per worker <sup>[26](#o26), [1](#o1)</sup>.
- **If the nulla osta is revoked through the employer's fault:** the employer must pay you an indemnity based on the CCNL wages it owes you <sup>[26](#o26)</sup>.
- **If you lose the job:** the rule that lets a worker who loses a job stay at least a year to look for another does not apply on a seasonal permit <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>; NASpI (unemployment pay) is not paid on a seasonal permit <sup>[65](#o65)</sup>.
- **Pension after you go home:** the rule that keeps a worker's Italian pension rights after going home for good, without a social security agreement, does not cover seasonal workers <sup>[157](#o157), [8](#o8)</sup>.

<a id="staying-on-another-season-or-a-non-seasonal-permit"></a>

### 4.7 Staying on: another season or a non-seasonal permit

1. <a id="staying-on-another-season-or-a-non-seasonal-permit-item-1"></a>**Take a second seasonal job.** Within the nine months, your permit can be extended for a new seasonal job with the same or another employer, if it starts within 60 days of the end of the last contract <sup>[26](#o26)</sup>.
2. <a id="staying-on-another-season-or-a-non-seasonal-permit-item-2"></a>**Come back on a multi-year nulla osta.** With a three-year seasonal nulla osta, the Embassy issues the visa for the second and third years when you show the employer's contract proposal <sup>[26](#o26)</sup>. In those later years a different employer can make the request <sup>[26](#o26)</sup>.
3. <a id="staying-on-another-season-or-a-non-seasonal-permit-item-3"></a>**Convert to a non-seasonal permit.** After at least three months of regular work, if an employer offers you a fixed-term or permanent contract, you can ask the SUI to convert your seasonal permit into a work permit <sup>[26](#o26), [190](#o190)</sup> (see [Changing visa type](#changing-visa-type)). By December 2025, 16,360 nulla osta had been issued on 32,016 such requests made in 2025 <sup>[N11](#n11)</sup>.
4. <a id="staying-on-another-season-or-a-non-seasonal-permit-item-4"></a>**Otherwise, go home** at the end of the nine months, unless you hold a permit for another reason <sup>[26](#o26)</sup>. Going home on time keeps your priority for the next season <sup>[16](#o16)</sup>. Italy closed seasonal work to Bangladeshis in 2012 after Bangladeshi farm workers did not go home after each season <sup>[N15 (2023)](#n15)</sup> (see [What closed it before](#what-closed-it-before)). A seasonal permit lasts at most nine months in twelve, and only a permit of at least one year lets you bring your family, so a seasonal worker cannot sponsor a wife or children <sup>[26](#o26), [184](#o184)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - What does converting a seasonal permit to a non-seasonal one cost, and how long does the SUI take in 2026?
> - What share of Bangladeshi seasonal workers go home at the end of the season?

<a id="caporalato-illegal-gangmasters"></a>

### 4.8 Caporalato: illegal gangmasters

Caporalato is when a caporale (gangmaster) recruits workers to work for others under exploitative conditions <sup>[92 (2016)](#o92)</sup>. Recent cases were around Latina (the Agro Pontino), near Naples and in Calabria <sup>[58](#o58), [N54](#n54), [N55](#n55)</sup>.

<a id="what-it-looks-like"></a>

#### 4.8.1 What it looks like

- **Near Naples (Aversa):** prosecutors found farm workers paid about EUR 2.70 (about Tk 380) an hour for 10-14 hours a day, carried packed into the cargo space of vans <sup>[58](#o58), [1](#o1)</sup>.
- **Calabria (Sibari plain), September 2026:** 18 Pakistanis, one Bangladeshi and one Indian were arrested in a caporalato inquiry <sup>[N54](#n54)</sup>. Farms allegedly paid EUR 40 (about Tk 5,600) cash for ten hours, handed to the gangmasters, who kept EUR 5 (about Tk 700) a day for transport and EUR 60 (about Tk 8,400) a month for rent <sup>[N54](#n54), [1](#o1)</sup>.
- **Latina:** Satnam Singh, an Indian worker employed off the books on a farm at Borgo Santa Maria, lost an arm to a machine on 19 June 2024 <sup>[N55](#n55)</sup>. On 8 July 2026 the Latina court sentenced the farm's owner to 16 years at first instance <sup>[N55](#n55)</sup>.
- **Fake hiring:** in June 2026, 30 people were arrested across Italy for false Decreto Flussi applications filed on the ALI portal through a CAF (centro di assistenza fiscale, a tax help office) in Taranto, mostly for people from Pakistan, Bangladesh and India, who paid up to EUR 6,500 (about Tk 9.1 lakh) for a nulla osta and visa <sup>[N23](#n23), [1](#o1)</sup>.

<a id="the-law"></a>

#### 4.8.2 The law

Law 199/2016 punishes both the recruiter and the employer who uses exploited workers with 1-6 years in prison and a fine of EUR 500-1,000 (about Tk 70,000-1.4 lakh) per worker, or 5-8 years with violence or threats <sup>[92 (2016)](#o92), [1](#o1)</sup>. Signs of exploitation include pay repeatedly far below the national contracts, and degrading working conditions, surveillance or housing <sup>[92 (2016)](#o92)</sup>.

<a id="if-you-are-exploited"></a>

#### 4.8.3 If you are exploited

Act in this order:

1. <a id="if-you-are-exploited-item-1"></a>**Call the anti-trafficking helpline, 800 290 290:** free, anonymous and open 24 hours a day, every day, for victims of trafficking or exploitation and anyone reporting it <sup>[191](#o191)</sup>. Its interpreters cover English, Arabic and ten other languages, not Bengali <sup>[191](#o191)</sup>.
2. <a id="if-you-are-exploited-item-2"></a>**Report it to the INL or the police** (see [If you aren't paid](#if-you-arent-paid)). The INL can pass the case to the prosecutor or the Questore (the head of the Questura) and give its opinion on a permit for you <sup>[174](#o174)</sup>.
3. <a id="if-you-are-exploited-item-3"></a>**Ask for the special permit for victims.** If police or prosecutors find violence, abuse or exploitation of a foreign worker who helps bring out the facts and identify those responsible, the Questore issues an Article 18-ter permit at once, on the prosecutor's proposal <sup>[174](#o174)</sup>. It is marked "casi speciali" (special cases), lasts one year, can be renewed and lets you work, employed or self-employed <sup>[174](#o174)</sup>. While you wait, the receipt for your application lets you stay and work <sup>[174](#o174)</sup>. When it expires it can be converted into a work permit outside the quotas <sup>[174](#o174)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Can the 800 290 290 helpline bring in a Bengali interpreter on request?
> - Which Bangladeshi community organisations in Latina, Naples and Rome help workers report a caporale?
> - How long does a worker wait for an Article 18-ter permit after reporting, and how many went to Bangladeshis in 2025 and 2026?

<a id="work-visa-domestic-and-care-worker"></a>

## 5 Work visa: domestic and care worker

This visa lets a family in Italy hire you as a colf (house worker) or badante (carer), both called collaboratori familiari (domestic workers) <sup>[188](#o188), [16](#o16), [153](#o153)</sup>. Italy allows 13,600 domestic and care workers in 2026, 14,000 in 2027 and 14,200 in 2028, inside the non-seasonal quota and from the same list of countries, which includes Bangladesh <sup>[188](#o188), [6](#o6), [187](#o187)</sup>. This quota is only for jobs in a household; care jobs in clinics or care homes use the general non-seasonal quota (see Work visa: Decreto Flussi worker (non-seasonal)) <sup>[16](#o16)</sup>. Separately, up to 10,000 carers a year can enter outside the quota until 2028 (see [The out-of-quota care route](#the-out-of-quota-care-route)) <sup>[27](#o27), [49](#o49)</sup>. As for seasonal work, a Bangladeshi worker's nulla osta always needs the Questura's approval and the INL's check of the employer <sup>[16](#o16)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - How many domestic-work nulla osta and visas went to Bangladeshis in 2025 and 2026?
> - Do Italian families hire Bangladeshi men as live-in carers, or mostly relatives already in Italy?

<a id="step-1-find-a-real-family-employer"></a>

### 5.1 Step 1: Find a real family employer

1. <a id="step-1-find-a-real-family-employer-item-1"></a>**Check the family can afford you.** An employer living alone needs taxable income of at least EUR 20,000 a year (about Tk 27.9 lakh), or EUR 27,000 (about Tk 37.7 lakh) if the household has more members <sup>[16](#o16), [1](#o1)</sup>. An employer who is ill or disabled and hires you for their own care needs no minimum income <sup>[16](#o16)</sup>.

   **Scams:** a worker who arrives for an employer that does not exist falls into irregular status, as with a fake farm job <sup>[N4](#n4)</sup> (see [Work visa: seasonal worker](#work-visa-seasonal-worker), [Step 1, item 1: Know who can hire you](#step-1-find-a-real-seasonal-employer-item-1)).

> **Need to know**
> - What does a domestic-work nulla osta sell for in Bangladesh in 2026?
> - Can a family in Italy hire a Bangladeshi relative as a domestic worker on this quota?

<a id="step-2-the-family-applies-on-click-day"></a>

### 5.2 Step 2: The family applies on click day

The family does this step; you can only check it was done <sup>[16](#o16), [6](#o6)</sup>.

1. <a id="step-2-the-family-applies-on-click-day-item-1"></a>**The family asks the job centre first.** It must ask the Centro per l'Impiego whether a worker already in Italy can fill the job; if the centre offers no one within 8 days, it can go ahead <sup>[16](#o16)</sup>.
2. <a id="step-2-the-family-applies-on-click-day-item-2"></a>**The family gets the job certified.** It needs an asseverazione (a labour consultant's or employers' organisation's certificate that the job meets the rules), unless the employer is not self-sufficient <sup>[16](#o16)</sup>.
3. <a id="step-2-the-family-applies-on-click-day-item-3"></a>**The family files form A-bis on the ALI portal** on click day: 18 February 2027 from 9:00 for the 2027 round <sup>[6](#o6)</sup>. The pay stated must follow the domestic-work CCNL and be no less than the assegno sociale (the state's minimum social allowance: EUR 7,002.97, about Tk 9.8 lakh, a year in 2025) <sup>[16](#o16), [1](#o1)</sup>. Pre-filling and the rest of the procedure: see ["Work visa: Decreto Flussi worker (non-seasonal)"](#work-visa-decreto-flussi-worker-non-seasonal), [Step 3: The employer gets your nulla osta](#step-3-the-employer-gets-your-nulla-osta).

> **Need to know**
> - Do families' A-bis applications for Bangladeshi workers get through the Questura's checks in 2026, and how long do they take?

<a id="step-3-visa-documents-and-arrival"></a>

### 5.3 Step 3: Visa, documents and arrival

1. <a id="step-3-visa-documents-and-arrival-item-1"></a>**Get the visa and fly** as in "Work visa: Decreto Flussi worker (non-seasonal)", [Step 4: Passport, police clearance and certificates](#step-4-passport-police-clearance-and-certificates) to [Step 6: Orientation, BMET clearance and the flight](#step-6-orientation-bmet-clearance-and-the-flight).
2. <a id="step-3-visa-documents-and-arrival-item-2"></a>**Check the family registers you with INPS.** If you start work before the contratto di soggiorno is signed, the family must file the hiring notice with INPS itself <sup>[16](#o16)</sup>. Contract, permit and codice fiscale: see ["Work visa: Decreto Flussi worker (non-seasonal)"](#work-visa-decreto-flussi-worker-non-seasonal), [Step 7: After arrival](#step-7-after-arrival).

<a id="your-rights-as-a-domestic-worker"></a>

### 5.4 Your rights as a domestic worker

The domestic-work CCNL (CCNL lavoro domestico, code H501 in the register of CNEL, the national council that files collective contracts) was renewed on 28 October 2025 and runs to 31 October 2028; it is signed by the families' associations FIDALDO (Nuova Collaborazione, Assindatcolf, ADLD and ADLC) and DOMINA, and by the unions FILCAMS CGIL, FISASCAT CISL, UILTUCS UIL and Federcolf <sup>[N37](#n37), [146](#o146)</sup>.

<a id="pay"></a>

#### 5.4.1 Pay

Minimum pay from 1 January 2026 <sup>[192](#o192)</sup>:

- **Live-in, level B** (general home helper): EUR 983.16 (about Tk 1.37 lakh) a month <sup>[192](#o192), [1](#o1)</sup>.
- **Live-in, level BS** (carer of a person who can look after themselves, or babysitter): EUR 1,053.39 (about Tk 1.47 lakh) a month <sup>[192](#o192), [N37](#n37), [1](#o1)</sup>.
- **Live-in, level CS** (untrained carer of a person who can't look after themselves): EUR 1,193.84 (about Tk 1.67 lakh) a month <sup>[192](#o192), [N37](#n37), [1](#o1)</sup>.
- **Live-in, level DS** (trained carer of a person who can't look after themselves): EUR 1,474.73 (about Tk 2.06 lakh) a month plus a EUR 207.69 (about Tk 29,000) allowance <sup>[192](#o192), [1](#o1)</sup>.
- **Not live-in:** EUR 7.45 (about Tk 1,040) an hour at level BS and EUR 8.30 (about Tk 1,160) at level CS <sup>[192](#o192), [1](#o1)</sup>.

Food and lodging are valued at EUR 6.66 (about Tk 930) a day <sup>[192](#o192), [1](#o1)</sup>. A 13th month's pay (tredicesima) is due in December <sup>[N37](#n37)</sup>.

<a id="hours-rest-and-leave-1"></a>

#### 5.4.2 Hours, rest and leave

- **Hours:** live-in, at most 10 non-consecutive hours a day and 54 a week; not live-in, at most 8 a day and 40 a week <sup>[N37](#n37)</sup>.
- **Rest:** live-in workers get at least 11 consecutive hours' rest a day and 36 hours a week, 24 of them on Sunday <sup>[N37](#n37)</sup>.
- **Overtime:** +25% between 6:00 and 22:00, +50% at night, +60% on Sundays and public holidays <sup>[N37](#n37)</sup>.
- **Leave:** 26 working days a year <sup>[N37](#n37)</sup>.
