- **Lodging and food:** a live-in worker must get lodging that protects dignity and privacy, and healthy, sufficient food <sup>[N37](#n37)</sup>.
- **Sickness:** your job is kept for 10 days with up to six months' service, 45 days up to two years, and 180 days after that <sup>[N37](#n37)</sup>.

<a id="probation-and-ending-the-job"></a>

#### 5.4.3 Probation and ending the job

- **Probation:** live-in workers have 30 working days' paid probation <sup>[N37](#n37)</sup>.
- **Notice:** for 25 hours a week or more, 15 days up to five years' service and 30 days after; half that if you resign <sup>[N37](#n37)</sup>.

For unpaid wages see [If you aren't paid](#if-you-arent-paid).

<a id="the-out-of-quota-care-route"></a>

### 5.5 The out-of-quota care route

Each year from 2025 to 2028, up to 10,000 workers can enter outside the quota to care for people with disabilities, very old people or children up to six <sup>[27](#o27), [49](#o49)</sup>. The family can't apply on its own: it applies through a registered employment agency (APL, agenzia per il lavoro) or an employers' association that signed the domestic-work CCNL, that is FIDALDO (Nuova Collaborazione, Assindatcolf, ADLD, ADLC) or DOMINA <sup>[27](#o27), [146](#o146)</sup>. It can't hire its own spouse or relatives up to the third degree this way <sup>[27](#o27)</sup>. For the first 12 months you may only do that care work, and changing employer needs the local labour inspectorate's prior approval <sup>[27](#o27)</sup>. In 2025, 238 carers entered this way, from 599 applications <sup>[N11](#n11)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Has any Bangladeshi entered on the out-of-quota care route, and which agencies (APL) filed for them?
> - What do those agencies charge the family or the worker?

<a id="work-visa-highly-skilled-and-out-of-quota-eu-blue-card-article-27"></a>

## 6 Work visa: highly skilled and out-of-quota (EU Blue Card, Article 27)

Article 27 of the TUI (Testo Unico Immigrazione, Italy's immigration law) lets some workers enter outside the yearly quota: managers and highly specialised staff, university teachers, translators, seafarers, posted workers, artists, athletes, journalists, digital nomads and nurses hired by health facilities <sup>[7](#o7)</sup>. The EU Blue Card is for highly qualified jobs; the Embassy and VFS Global call its nulla osta code "L/N" <sup>[66](#o66), [29](#o29), [111](#o111)</sup>.

<a id="who-qualifies-for-the-blue-card"></a>

### 6.1 Who qualifies for the Blue Card

You need one of these:

- a degree from a course of at least three years <sup>[66](#o66)</sup>;
- at least five years' experience at a comparable level in the job's field <sup>[66](#o66)</sup>;
- for ICT (information and communications technology) managers and specialists, three years' experience in the last seven <sup>[66](#o66)</sup>.

The employer needs a contract or binding offer of at least six months, at pay no lower than the CCNL and the average gross annual pay published by ISTAT (Italy's national statistics office) <sup>[66](#o66)</sup>. The visa is strictly for highly qualified workers recruited for highly qualified jobs <sup>[33](#o33)</sup>.

<a id="step-1-the-employer-gets-your-ln-nulla-osta"></a>

### 6.2 Step 1: The employer gets your L/N nulla osta

1. <a id="step-1-the-employer-gets-your-ln-nulla-osta-item-1"></a>**The employer applies to the SUI,** which issues or refuses the nulla osta within 30 days <sup>[66](#o66)</sup>. The employer's declarations are checked for truthfulness <sup>[49](#o49)</sup>.
2. <a id="step-1-the-employer-gets-your-ln-nulla-osta-item-2"></a>**Take the L/N nulla osta to a VFS centre, walk-in.** No webform booking is needed: bring a printed copy of the nulla osta and a copy of your passport data page; the Embassy checks the nulla osta and emails you a date to submit the application <sup>[111](#o111)</sup>. VFS addresses and hours: see ["Work visa: Decreto Flussi worker (non-seasonal)"](#work-visa-decreto-flussi-worker-non-seasonal), [Step 5: The visa at the Italian Embassy in Dhaka, and checking it is real](#step-5-the-visa-at-the-italian-embassy-in-dhaka-and-checking-it-is-real).

**Scams:** the Embassy has warned against brokers selling a "Nulla Osta L/N code - carta blu" <sup>[29](#o29)</sup>. Very few Bangladeshi L/N applicants have succeeded, most because they were not highly qualified, and a valid L/N nulla osta does not guarantee a visa <sup>[29](#o29)</sup>.

<a id="step-2-the-visa-at-vfs"></a>

### 6.3 Step 2: The visa at VFS

1. <a id="step-2-the-visa-at-vfs-item-1"></a>**Submit the application at VFS** on the date in the Embassy's email <sup>[111](#o111)</sup>. You'll need:

   - the original national ("D") visa form, filled in in English or Italian and signed <sup>[33](#o33)</sup>;
   - a passport valid at least 15 months from the application date, with two blank pages <sup>[33](#o33)</sup>;
   - a copy of the nulla osta, with your name, surname, sex and date of birth checked to avoid long delays <sup>[33](#o33)</sup>;
   - your degree with a dichiarazione di valore (the Italian Embassy's statement of the degree's value) or a CIMEA certificate (from Italy's centre for recognising foreign qualifications), or contracts or payslips proving five years in the sector, translated and apostilled <sup>[33](#o33)</sup>.

   Fees, photos and fingerprints are as in "Work visa: Decreto Flussi worker (non-seasonal)", [Step 5: The visa at the Italian Embassy in Dhaka, and checking it is real](#step-5-the-visa-at-the-italian-embassy-in-dhaka-and-checking-it-is-real).
2. <a id="step-2-the-visa-at-vfs-item-2"></a>**Follow the after-arrival steps** in "Work visa: Decreto Flussi worker (non-seasonal)", [Step 7: After arrival](#step-7-after-arrival). A permit for managers and specialised workers under Article 27 carries a EUR 100 (about Tk 14,000) contribution <sup>[121](#o121), [1](#o1)</sup>. For the first 12 months, changing employer needs the labour office's prior approval <sup>[66](#o66)</sup>. You may bring your family whatever the length of your permit (see [Family reunification](#family-reunification)) <sup>[66](#o66)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - How many Blue Card visas did the Embassy issue to Bangladeshis in 2025 and 2026, and in which jobs?

<a id="the-article-23-route-training-in-bangladesh-first"></a>

### 6.4 The Article 23 route: training in Bangladesh first

Workers who complete vocational and civic-language training in Bangladesh, in programmes approved by Italian ministries, can get a work visa on that basis <sup>[83](#o83)</sup>. The nulla osta deadline for them is 30 days, and they may ask for the visa up to 12 months after the course, until the end of 2027 <sup>[50](#o50)</sup>. Several hundred Bangladeshis a year now go legally through these pre-departure training projects <sup>[12](#o12)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Which training projects in Bangladesh qualify under Article 23 in 2026, who runs them, and how does a worker enrol?

<a id="self-employment-visa"></a>

## 7 Self-employment visa

Italy admits 650 self-employed workers in 2027, from all countries together <sup>[6](#o6)</sup>. You must show enough money, the qualifications Italian law requires for the activity, suitable housing and lawful yearly income above the level that exempts people from health charges <sup>[193](#o193)</sup>. The Embassy decides within 120 days, and the visa must be used within 180 days of issue <sup>[193](#o193)</sup>. The permit lasts up to 2 years, and each renewal up to 3 <sup>[9](#o9)</sup>. A self-employment permit of at least one year lets you bring your family, but for a wife you must first have lived legally in Italy for two years without a break (see [Family reunification](#family-reunification)) <sup>[184](#o184)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Has any Bangladeshi obtained an Italian self-employment visa in 2025 or 2026, and in which category?
> - What do agents charge for a "consultant" self-employment nulla osta, and are fake ones sold?

<a id="step-1-the-nulla-osta-and-certificates-in-italy"></a>

### 7.1 Step 1: The nulla osta and certificates in Italy

The Embassy's checklist covers consultants and officers of an existing Italian company <sup>[106](#o106), [194](#o194)</sup>.

1. <a id="step-1-the-nulla-osta-and-certificates-in-italy-item-1"></a>**Get these papers from Italy.** You'll need:

   - a nulla osta from the Questura, issued within the 90 days before you apply <sup>[106](#o106)</sup>;
   - a certificate from the competent Italian authority for the activity, no older than three months <sup>[193](#o193)</sup>;
   - a certificate from the Direzione Provinciale del Lavoro (the provincial labour office) that the contract is not employment <sup>[194](#o194)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Who asks the Questura for a self-employment nulla osta (the company or the worker's representative), with which papers, and how long it takes?

<a id="step-2-the-visa-at-vfs-1"></a>

### 7.2 Step 2: The visa at VFS

1. <a id="step-2-the-visa-at-vfs-1-item-1"></a>**Gather the documents.** You'll need:

   - the original national ("D") visa form, filled in in English or Italian <sup>[194](#o194)</sup>;
   - a passport valid at least 3 months after you leave the Schengen area, with two blank pages, and a copy of all its pages <sup>[194](#o194)</sup>;
   - the papers from [Step 1: The nulla osta and certificates in Italy](#step-1-the-nulla-osta-and-certificates-in-italy);
   - a Chamber of Commerce certificate that the company has been active for at least three years, and its visura camerale (registration extract) <sup>[106](#o106), [194](#o194)</sup>;
   - the company legal representative's guarantee of pay of at least EUR 8,500 (about Tk 11.9 lakh) a year <sup>[106](#o106), [1](#o1)</sup>;
   - the company's last balance sheet or tax return <sup>[194](#o194)</sup>;
   - 6 months of your personal bank statements from a bank in Bangladesh <sup>[106](#o106)</sup>;
   - proof of housing: a purchase or rental contract, or a housing declaration from an Italian or a legal resident, with a copy of their ID <sup>[194](#o194)</sup>;
   - travel insurance valid across the Schengen area for medical, hospital and repatriation costs up to EUR 30,000 (about Tk 41.9 lakh) for the first 3 months <sup>[194](#o194), [1](#o1)</sup>;
   - civil-status papers: if married, the nikahnama translated into English and apostilled (one apostille per document); a talaknama if divorced; the spouse's death certificate if widowed <sup>[106](#o106), [194](#o194)</sup>;
   - a flight booking with a PNR (booking reference) <sup>[194](#o194)</sup>.

2. <a id="step-2-the-visa-at-vfs-1-item-2"></a>**Submit at VFS and pay** in cash the EUR 116 visa fee (Tk 16,240) and the EUR 38 VFS charge (Tk 5,320), at the Embassy's rate of EUR 1 = Tk 140, plus a Tk 270 bank draft charge <sup>[2](#o2)</sup>. VFS addresses, hours and booking: see ["Work visa: Decreto Flussi worker (non-seasonal)"](#work-visa-decreto-flussi-worker-non-seasonal), [Step 5: The visa at the Italian Embassy in Dhaka, and checking it is real](#step-5-the-visa-at-the-italian-embassy-in-dhaka-and-checking-it-is-real). By law the Embassy has 120 days to issue a self-employment visa <sup>[113](#o113)</sup>.

<a id="student-visa"></a>

## 8 Student visa

The student visa is a type D (national, long-stay) visa called "Immatricolazione Università" (university enrolment), valid 365 days with multiple entries <sup>[195](#o195)</sup>. Italy issued about 500 study visas to Bangladeshis in 2025 and expects to issue more in 2026, but the Italian ambassador says many more would not be sustainable because universities have few places for foreign students <sup>[52](#o52)</sup>. A university's acceptance does not guarantee a visa appointment or a visa: the Italian Embassy in Dhaka also judges the "migration risk" from the family's money, your past studies and how well the course fits them <sup>[195](#o195)</sup>.

For courses starting in 2026/27, visa applications must reach the Embassy by 30 November 2026, and universities may set earlier dates for each course <sup>[196](#o196), [195](#o195)</sup>. For 2027/28 the deadline is 31 October 2027 <sup>[195](#o195)</sup>.

<a id="step-1-choose-a-course-and-check-you-qualify"></a>

### 8.1 Step 1: Choose a course and check you qualify

1. <a id="step-1-choose-a-course-and-check-you-qualify-item-1"></a>**Check the entry level.** A bachelor's course needs a school-leaving certificate after at least 12 years of schooling (in Bangladesh, the HSC, Higher Secondary Certificate) <sup>[195](#o195)</sup>.
2. <a id="step-1-choose-a-course-and-check-you-qualify-item-2"></a>**Check the language.** Courses taught in Italian need Italian at level B2 or above, tested by the university <sup>[195](#o195)</sup>. For courses in English, the Dhaka Embassy accepts only IELTS, with a score of at least 6, issued within the two years before you apply <sup>[197](#o197)</sup>. The certificate must come from a recognised institute or university and cover all four skills (listening, reading, speaking, writing) <sup>[196](#o196), [195](#o195)</sup>.
3. <a id="step-1-choose-a-course-and-check-you-qualify-item-3"></a>**Check the money.** You must show EUR 10,179.85 (about Tk 14.2 lakh) a year, from lawful, traceable sources, preferably your own or your family's <sup>[195](#o195), [1](#o1)</sup>. The Embassy accepts money held by you or by your parents, grandparents, brothers and sisters, uncles and aunts, or cousins <sup>[196](#o196)</sup>. It checks where the money came from, the account's movements, and whether the sum fits the family's income and assets <sup>[195](#o195)</sup>. The bank statements must cover the last 12 months, and the sponsor's money must be explained by salary, property or savings held for some time <sup>[197](#o197)</sup>. A scholarship does not replace this proof <sup>[197](#o197)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - What balance and account history does the Dhaka Embassy accept in practice for a sponsor's FDR (fixed deposit) or savings account?
> - What do "bank solvency" agents in Dhaka charge to place money in a family account before a student visa application, and how often does the Embassy catch it?

<a id="step-2-get-your-certificates-checked"></a>

### 8.2 Step 2: Get your certificates checked

The visa needs your HSC (or bachelor's) certificate backed either by statements from CIMEA (Italy's official centre for comparing foreign qualifications) or by a Declaration of Value (Dichiarazione di Valore, DoV: the Italian Embassy's statement of what your certificate is worth in Italy) <sup>[195](#o195), [196](#o196)</sup>. Whichever the university uses, the Embassy needs a DoV or a CIMEA statement that also confirms the certificate is genuine <sup>[198](#o198)</sup>. From 2026/27 a university can no longer accept you "with reservation" while these papers are missing <sup>[198](#o198)</sup>.

1. <a id="step-2-get-your-certificates-checked-item-1"></a>**Apostille your certificates first,** following "Work visa: Decreto Flussi worker (non-seasonal)", [Step 4: Passport, police clearance and certificates](#step-4-passport-police-clearance-and-certificates). The Italian Embassy no longer legalises Bangladeshi documents; they need an apostille (the international stamp of authenticity) from Bangladesh's Ministry of Foreign Affairs (MoFA) through the MyGov portal, at Tk 200 a page, in 5 working days, after your Education Board and the Ministry of Education verify the SSC (Secondary School Certificate) and HSC <sup>[199](#o199), [86](#o86), [103](#o103)</sup>.
2. <a id="step-2-get-your-certificates-checked-item-2"></a>**Either: apply to CIMEA yourself** on [cimea-diplome.it](https://cimea-diplome.it/), its only official channel <sup>[200](#o200)</sup>. Use the Comparability & Verification service, meant for qualifications from countries outside the Lisbon Recognition Convention (the European treaty on recognising qualifications) <sup>[201](#o201)</sup>:
   - **Cost:** EUR 300 (about Tk 41,900) per qualification, which is EUR 245.90 plus VAT <sup>[201](#o201), [1](#o1)</sup>.
   - **Time:** 60 working days; the comparability part can be done in 30 working days for EUR 100 (about Tk 14,000) more, but the verification part always takes the ordinary time <sup>[201](#o201), [1](#o1)</sup>.
   - **Who applies:** only the certificate holder; CIMEA takes no requests through agents and prohibits brokerage <sup>[201](#o201), [200](#o200)</sup>.
3. <a id="step-2-get-your-certificates-checked-item-3"></a>**Or: ask for a DoV** from the Italian Embassy <sup>[196](#o196)</sup>. The Embassy's 2026 fee list prices a DoV at EUR 50 (Tk 7,000) and marks it free for study in Italy <sup>[108](#o108)</sup>.

**Check it is real:** a genuine CIMEA statement carries a QR code and a verification link recorded on a blockchain; fake CIMEA certificates have been submitted to Italian universities <sup>[200](#o200)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - How long does the Dhaka Embassy take to issue a DoV for an HSC or bachelor's certificate in 2026, and which documents does VFS ask for?
> - Is the DoV still free for students at the counter, or does VFS add its own charge?

<a id="step-3-pre-enrol-on-universitaly"></a>

### 8.3 Step 3: Pre-enrol on Universitaly

1. <a id="step-3-pre-enrol-on-universitaly-item-1"></a>**Apply to the university first, by its own deadline.** Each university posts on its own portal, course by course, the date by which it needs your visa application, which may be earlier than the national deadline <sup>[195](#o195)</sup>.
2. <a id="step-3-pre-enrol-on-universitaly-item-2"></a>**Pre-enrol on [universitaly.it](https://www.universitaly.it/),** the free official portal of Italy's Ministry of University and Research; no other channel is accepted <sup>[195](#o195)</sup>.
3. <a id="step-3-pre-enrol-on-universitaly-item-3"></a>**Wait for the university to validate your pre-enrolment.** Only validated students can apply for the visa <sup>[195](#o195)</sup>. From 2026/27 each university may validate only up to its places for foreign students plus 20% <sup>[195](#o195)</sup>. Uni-Italia, a non-profit association, gives guidance on Universitaly through its centres abroad <sup>[195](#o195)</sup>.
4. <a id="step-3-pre-enrol-on-universitaly-item-4"></a>**Do not pay tuition yet:** university fees are normally paid only after the visa is issued <sup>[195](#o195)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Does Uni-Italia have a centre or desk serving Bangladesh, and how do students reach it?

<a id="step-4-apply-for-the-visa-at-vfs"></a>

### 8.4 Step 4: Apply for the visa at VFS

1. <a id="step-4-apply-for-the-visa-at-vfs-item-1"></a>**Wait for VFS's email.** VFS Global emails students registered on Universitaly, at the address used there, to book the appointment <sup>[196](#o196)</sup>. Appointments are always free, also through VFS <sup>[199](#o199)</sup>.
2. <a id="step-4-apply-for-the-visa-at-vfs-item-2"></a>**You'll need** (the Embassy's checklist):
   - the type D visa form, one ICAO photo (the international passport-photo standard: 3.5 x 4.0 cm, white background), and your passport (original and one copy), valid at least 3 months beyond the visa, with two blank pages <sup>[197](#o197)</sup>;
   - the Universitaly pre-enrolment receipt showing the tuition fees <sup>[197](#o197)</sup>;
   - the DoV, or the receipt of your DoV application, or a verifiable CIMEA certificate <sup>[196](#o196), [197](#o197)</sup>;
   - the IELTS (6 or more) or Italian B2 certificate <sup>[197](#o197), [195](#o195)</sup>;
   - 12 months' bank statements of the student and the sponsor, and the birth certificate linking the sponsor to you, apostilled by MoFA <sup>[197](#o197)</sup>;
   - proof of accommodation: a booking of at least 30 days, a lease, or a hosting declaration by a legal resident of Italy with a copy of their ID <sup>[197](#o197)</sup>;
   - a one-way flight booking <sup>[197](#o197)</sup>, and health insurance valid in the whole Schengen area for your whole stay, covering medical, hospital and repatriation costs of at least EUR 30,000 (about Tk 41.9 lakh) <sup>[197](#o197), [202](#o202), [1](#o1)</sup>;
   - if married, divorced or widowed: the nikahnama (Muslim marriage contract), talaknama (divorce deed) or spouse's death certificate, in English and apostilled <sup>[203](#o203)</sup>.

   Applying for a university residence place is not proof of accommodation <sup>[195](#o195)</sup>.
3. <a id="step-4-apply-for-the-visa-at-vfs-item-3"></a>**Give your fingerprints:** since 11 January 2025 they are taken for national visas too <sup>[195](#o195)</sup>.
4. <a id="step-4-apply-for-the-visa-at-vfs-item-4"></a>**Pay in cash, in Taka** <sup>[2](#o2)</sup>:
   - visa fee: Tk 7,000 (EUR 50) <sup>[2](#o2)</sup>;
   - VFS service charge: Tk 5,320 (EUR 38) <sup>[2](#o2)</sup>;
   - bank draft charge: Tk 270 <sup>[2](#o2)</sup>.

   Depending on the course's length the Embassy may grant the visa free, and then the VFS charge is waived too <sup>[2](#o2)</sup>.
5. <a id="step-4-apply-for-the-visa-at-vfs-item-5"></a>**If your admission or language test is in Italy,** you get a type D visa with a fixed validity of 100 days, and you do not need to come home for a new visa after passing <sup>[195](#o195)</sup>.

**Check it is real:** VFS only handles the logistics and has no say in the decision; neither VFS nor the Embassy asks for money beyond the published fees <sup>[11](#o11)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Which course lengths get a free study visa at the Dhaka Embassy?
> - How long does the Dhaka Embassy take to decide a study visa in 2026?

<a id="step-5-after-you-arrive"></a>

### 8.5 Step 5: After you arrive

1. <a id="step-5-after-you-arrive-item-1"></a>**Apply for the study permit within 8 working days,** at the Questura (the provincial police headquarters that issues residence permits) of the city where you will live, with the kit from a post office or a desk at the university <sup>[195](#o195)</sup>. The post office receipt proves you are in Italy legally until the permit is ready <sup>[195](#o195)</sup>. Patronati (free advice offices run by trade unions) and some municipalities help fill in the kit free <sup>[195](#o195)</sup>. Information: poliziadistato.it, portaleimmigrazione.it, toll-free 803160 <sup>[195](#o195)</sup>.
2. <a id="step-5-after-you-arrive-item-2"></a>**Enrol at the university by 31 January.** From 31 January of the academic year the visas of students not yet enrolled are revoked and the Questura is told <sup>[195](#o195)</sup>.
3. <a id="step-5-after-you-arrive-item-3"></a>**For the codice fiscale (tax code), the health service and a bank account,** follow "Work visa: Decreto Flussi worker (non-seasonal)", [Step 7: After arrival](#step-7-after-arrival).

<a id="how-long-you-can-stay-1"></a>

### 8.6 How long you can stay

1. <a id="how-long-you-can-stay-1-item-1"></a>**Renew the permit** at the Questura at least 60 days before it expires <sup>[195](#o195)</sup>.
2. <a id="how-long-you-can-stay-1-item-2"></a>**Pass your exams:** renewal needs one exam passed in the first year and at least two in each later year <sup>[195](#o195)</sup>. Permits are not issued for more than three years beyond the course's length <sup>[195](#o195)</sup>.
3. <a id="how-long-you-can-stay-1-item-3"></a>**Changing course:** you may move to another university degree with approval, but not to a private course <sup>[195](#o195)</sup>. Formally withdrawing from your studies ends your right to stay, and you must leave Italy <sup>[195](#o195)</sup>.
4. <a id="how-long-you-can-stay-1-item-4"></a>**After graduating:** see Changing visa type <sup>[204](#o204)</sup>.

<a id="can-you-work"></a>

### 8.7 Can you work?

A study permit allows a job of up to 20 hours a week, and at most 1,040 hours a year <sup>[205](#o205)</sup>. No contract may go over 20 hours a week, even within the 1,040-hour yearly limit, and working more needs a conversion to a work permit first (see [Changing visa type](#changing-visa-type)) <sup>[206 (2022)](#o206)</sup>.

<a id="grants-for-students-from-poor-countries"></a>

### 8.8 Grants for students from poor countries

Bangladesh is on Italy's 2026/27 list of "particularly poor countries" <sup>[207](#o207)</sup>. For students from these countries, the regional grants for university students (DSU, diritto allo studio, the right to study) judge need by a certificate from the Italian Embassy that the student does not come from a notably high-income, high-status family <sup>[208](#o208)</sup>. Being eligible for a grant does not count as money for the visa until the grant is formally awarded <sup>[195](#o195)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - How does a Bangladeshi student get the Italian Embassy's "not from a high-income family" certificate for DSU in 2026, and how long does it take?

<a id="bringing-your-family-1"></a>

### 8.9 Bringing your family

A student whose permit lasts at least one year can bring children under 18 once the income and housing rules are met, and a wife or husband after two years' legal residence in Italy (see [Family reunification](#family-reunification)) <sup>[184](#o184), [71](#o71)</sup>.

<a id="if-the-visa-is-refused"></a>

### 8.10 If the visa is refused

The refusal must give reasons, and you can appeal to the Regional Administrative Court (TAR, Tribunale Amministrativo Regionale) of Lazio within 60 days <sup>[114](#o114)</sup>. Nobody has a right to a visa, only a "legitimate interest" the court can protect <sup>[114](#o114)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - How many Bangladeshi study visa applications did the Dhaka Embassy refuse in 2025, and for what main reasons?

<a id="scams-1"></a>

### 8.11 Scams

<a id="done-to-students"></a>

#### 8.11.1 Done to students

Pre-enrolment on Universitaly is free, CIMEA takes no requests through agents, and visa appointments are free, so you can do all three yourself <sup>[195](#o195), [201](#o201), [199](#o199)</sup>. The Embassy itself has warned students not to use intermediaries or brokers <sup>[209 (2019)](#o209)</sup>. Fake CIMEA certificates have been submitted to Italian universities; check the QR code (see [Student visa](#student-visa), [Step 2: Get your certificates checked](#step-2-get-your-certificates-checked)) <sup>[200](#o200)</sup>. If an agent asks for money beyond the published fees or holds your passport, report it (see [Contacts in Bangladesh](#contacts-in-bangladesh)) <sup>[11](#o11)</sup>.

<a id="tried-by-applicants"></a>

#### 8.11.2 Tried by applicants

Forged or altered documents lead to immediate refusal, and the Embassy has said applicants with fake papers are banned from applying and reported to the Italian and Bangladeshi police <sup>[N56](#n56), [209 (2019)](#o209)</sup>. A student visa used only to get into Italy is revoked if you do not enrol by 31 January <sup>[195](#o195)</sup>.

<a id="visit-visa-schengen"></a>

## 9 Visit visa (Schengen)

A Schengen visa (type C, short stay) allows up to 90 days in any 180 days in Italy and the other Schengen countries <sup>[210](#o210)</sup>. It covers tourism, visiting family or friends, business and medical treatment <sup>[203](#o203), [211](#o211), [212](#o212)</sup>. It never allows work, study or applying to stay in Italy (see [Changing visa type](#changing-visa-type)) <sup>[31](#o31)</sup>. Bangladesh's Ministry of Expatriates' Welfare warns people not to go to work on a tourist or visit visa on a broker's promise <sup>[73](#o73)</sup>.

Most Bangladeshi applicants are refused. In 2025 Italy's consulate in Dhaka received 6,521 Schengen applications and refused 3,948 (60%), after refusing 65% in 2024 <sup>[32](#o32), [213](#o213)</sup>. Italy refused 11% worldwide, and its consulates in New Delhi, Karachi and Colombo refused 17%, 40% and 38% <sup>[32](#o32)</sup>. In Dhaka, Germany refused 30%, Spain 40%, France 47% and Sweden 74% <sup>[32](#o32)</sup>. The Italian ambassador says the rate is high because many people who try to enter Italy illegally through these visas submit fake documents <sup>[52](#o52)</sup>.

<a id="step-1-prepare-the-papers"></a>

### 9.1 Step 1: Prepare the papers

You'll need (the Embassy's tourism checklist):

- the Schengen visa form, filled in in English or Italian and signed <sup>[203](#o203)</sup>;
- one ICAO photo (the international passport-photo standard): 3.5 x 4.0 cm, colour, white background, under 6 months old <sup>[203](#o203)</sup>;
- a passport valid at least three months beyond the visa, with two blank visa pages <sup>[203](#o203)</sup>;
- your own bank statement from a bank in Bangladesh for the last 6 months <sup>[203](#o203)</sup>;
- if employed: your employer's original letter giving your salary, years of service, leave approval and confirmation that your job is kept for your return <sup>[203](#o203)</sup>;
- if self-employed: trade licence, incorporation certificate, tax return and TIN (Taxpayer Identification Number), and 6 months' company bank statements <sup>[203](#o203)</sup>;
- a return flight booking with a PNR (the booking reference) <sup>[203](#o203)</sup>;
- a hotel booking, or other proof of where you will stay, for the whole trip <sup>[203](#o203)</sup>;
- travel insurance for all Schengen countries and the whole stay, covering at least EUR 30,000 (about Tk 41.9 lakh) <sup>[203](#o203), [1](#o1)</sup>;
- if married, divorced or widowed: nikahnama (marriage contract), talaknama (divorce deed) or death certificate, in English and apostilled, one apostille per document <sup>[203](#o203)</sup>.

The Embassy may ask for more <sup>[203](#o203)</sup>. The money you must show for a visit is set by an Interior Ministry table <sup>[214](#o214), [1](#o1)</sup>. For one person it is:

- 1 to 5 days: EUR 269.60 in all (about Tk 37,600) <sup>[214](#o214), [1](#o1)</sup>;
- 6 to 10 days: EUR 44.93 a day (about Tk 6,300) <sup>[214](#o214), [1](#o1)</sup>;
- 11 to 20 days: EUR 51.64 (about Tk 7,200) plus EUR 36.67 a day (about Tk 5,100) <sup>[214](#o214), [1](#o1)</sup>;
- over 20 days: EUR 206.58 (about Tk 28,800) plus EUR 27.89 a day (about Tk 3,900) <sup>[214](#o214), [1](#o1)</sup>.

**Visiting family or friends in Italy:** add the invitation letter in the Embassy's format, proof of the relationship, the host's ID and, if the host is not Italian, their residence permit <sup>[203](#o203)</sup>.

**Business:** add the Italian company's invitation in the Embassy's format on company paper, its Chamber of Commerce registration (Visura Camerale) no older than 6 months, and proof of recent trade such as bills of lading and receipts <sup>[211](#o211)</sup>.

**Medical treatment:** add the hospital's appointment confirmation, the expected length of treatment, and proof you can pay for it <sup>[215 (2017)](#o215), [212](#o212)</sup>. For treatment paid by the Italian state, add the Region's act or a Ministry of Health authorisation <sup>[212](#o212)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Do Dhaka agents' "visa files" (bank solvency, employer letters, hotel bookings) for Italian tourist visas cost Tk 1 to 3 lakh in 2026, and what share are refused?

<a id="step-2-book-and-apply-at-vfs"></a>

### 9.2 Step 2: Book and apply at VFS

1. <a id="step-2-book-and-apply-at-vfs-item-1"></a>**Apply only through VFS Global,** the Embassy's only visa service provider in Bangladesh <sup>[216](#o216)</sup>. Apply no more than six months and, as a rule, at least 15 calendar days before the trip; the decision is due within 15 days, extendable to 45 in specific cases <sup>[113](#o113)</sup>. Book at [visa.vfsglobal.com/bgd/en/ita](https://visa.vfsglobal.com/bgd/en/ita/), call +880 9606777666 or +880 966611384 (from mobiles only), or email info.itbd@vfshelpline.com <sup>[216](#o216)</sup>. The appointment is free <sup>[199](#o199)</sup>.
2. <a id="step-2-book-and-apply-at-vfs-item-2"></a>**Go to a VFS centre,** Sunday to Thursday, 9 am to 12 noon <sup>[216](#o216)</sup>:
   - **Dhaka:** Nafi Tower (4th and 5th floor), 53 South Gulshan Avenue, Gulshan 1, Dhaka 1212 <sup>[216](#o216)</sup>;
   - **Chattogram:** World Trade Center, 102/103 Agrabad C/A, Commerce College Road, Chattogram 4100 <sup>[216](#o216)</sup>;
   - **Sylhet:** Nirvana Inn Complex, 4th floor, Ramer Dighir Par, Mirzajungle Road, Sylhet 3100 <sup>[216](#o216)</sup>.
3. <a id="step-2-book-and-apply-at-vfs-item-3"></a>**Give your fingerprints** at the centre <sup>[195](#o195)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - Are fingerprints taken at VFS for every Schengen applicant, and are children under 12 exempt as for family visas?

4. <a id="step-2-book-and-apply-at-vfs-item-4"></a>**Pay in cash, in Taka,** at the Embassy's rate of EUR 1 = Tk 140: EUR 90 (Tk 12,600), or EUR 45 (Tk 6,300) for a child aged 6 to 12 <sup>[217](#o217), [108](#o108)</sup>.
5. <a id="step-2-book-and-apply-at-vfs-item-5"></a>**Questions** go to the Embassy's visa office: visti.dhaka@esteri.it <sup>[216](#o216)</sup>. The Embassy also takes Schengen visa applications for Malta and Slovakia <sup>[216](#o216)</sup>.

**Check it is real:** VFS only handles the logistics and has no say in the decision; neither VFS nor the Embassy asks for money beyond the published fees or keeps a passport against your will <sup>[11](#o11)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - What is VFS's service charge in Taka for an Italian Schengen visa in 2026, and how long is the wait for an appointment?
> - How many working days does the Dhaka Embassy actually take to decide a tourist visa in 2026, against the 15-day legal limit?
> - Is there an official way to check an Italian Schengen visa sticker's number from Bangladesh, beyond showing it at VFS or the Embassy?

<a id="step-3-at-the-border"></a>

### 9.3 Step 3: At the border

1. <a id="step-3-at-the-border-item-1"></a>**A visa does not guarantee entry:** border police can refuse you if you cannot show the purpose of the trip and that the conditions are met <sup>[210](#o210)</sup>. Carry the return ticket, the hotel booking or invitation, the money, and the EUR 30,000 insurance <sup>[218](#o218)</sup>.
2. <a id="step-3-at-the-border-item-2"></a>**Get your passport stamped** when you enter the Schengen area from Bangladesh: the stamp is your "declaration of presence" <sup>[210](#o210)</sup>. If you arrive through another Schengen country, declare your presence at the Questura (the provincial police headquarters) within 8 days; a hotel's registration counts <sup>[210](#o210)</sup>.
3. <a id="step-3-at-the-border-item-3"></a>**The emergency number in Italy is 112** <sup>[210](#o210)</sup>.

**Changing planes in Italy without entering:** Bangladeshis need an airport transit visa even for this, unless they hold a valid Schengen or national visa or residence permit, or another listed exemption <sup>[78](#o78)</sup>.

<a id="can-you-work-1"></a>

### 9.4 Can you work?

No: a visit visa does not allow work, study or applying to stay in Italy <sup>[31](#o31)</sup>. A visitor who overstays can be expelled (see [What can get you jailed or deported](#what-can-get-you-jailed-or-deported)) <sup>[210](#o210)</sup>.

<a id="if-the-visa-is-refused-1"></a>

### 9.5 If the visa is refused

The refusal letter must give reasons, in a language you understand or else in English, French, Spanish or Arabic; you can appeal to the TAR (Regional Administrative Court) of Lazio within 60 days of notification, and the appeal must also be served on the Avvocatura dello Stato (the State Legal Service) <sup>[114](#o114), [115](#o115)</sup>.

<a id="scams-2"></a>

### 9.6 Scams

<a id="done-to-travellers"></a>

#### 9.6.1 Done to travellers

In February 2025 Rome prosecutors obtained a custody order against five people, among them two Italian Foreign Ministry employees who had served at the Embassy in Dhaka and two Bangladeshis; their syndicate allegedly sold tourist visas for EUR 7,000 (about Tk 9.8 lakh) and work visas for EUR 15,000 <sup>[51](#o51), [N25](#n25), [N57](#n57), [1](#o1)</sup>. Foreign Ministry inspections since 2023 have uncovered criminal networks in Italy that profit from fraudulent tourist visas <sup>[51](#o51)</sup>. Anyone who charges you for a VFS appointment is cheating you: appointments are free <sup>[199](#o199)</sup>.

<a id="tried-by-travellers"></a>

#### 9.6.2 Tried by travellers

Fake or altered documents lead to immediate refusal <sup>[N56](#n56)</sup>. Two men caught at Rome airport with fake Schengen visas in May 2026 had agreed to pay a ring Tk 30 lakh each and had paid Tk 20 lakh in advance <sup>[N44](#n44)</sup>. In June 2026 the CID (Bangladesh police's Criminal Investigation Department) arrested a Biman ground officer who allegedly helped three passengers fly to Italy on fake Schengen visas; two were caught at Rome airport and sent back <sup>[N58](#n58)</sup>. Immigration police describe a "page substitution" ring that cuts a genuine Italian visa page out of one passport and puts it into a look-alike's passport <sup>[N45](#n45)</sup>. The police Special Branch stopped 15,516 passengers at Bangladesh's airports in 2025, and Italy is among the countries it links to visa fraud <sup>[N45](#n45)</sup>.

<a id="family-reunification"></a>

## 10 Family reunification

Ricongiungimento familiare (family reunification) is how a foreigner living legally in Italy brings a wife or husband, children or parents from Bangladesh <sup>[71](#o71)</sup>. It runs in two halves: you apply in Italy for a nulla osta (entry clearance) from the SUI (Sportello Unico per l'Immigrazione, the immigration desk at the Prefettura, the central government's office in each province), then your family applies with it for a visa at VFS Global in Dhaka <sup>[71](#o71), [185](#o185)</sup>. The Embassy says it issues about 12,000 to 13,000 family visas a year, with few refusals on new applications <sup>[30](#o30)</sup>. Family members can work as soon as they arrive <sup>[30](#o30), [186](#o186)</sup>.

<a id="who-can-come"></a>

### 10.1 Who can come

- **Your wife or husband,** aged 18 or over and not legally separated <sup>[71](#o71)</sup>. If you already live in Italy with one wife, a second wife cannot come <sup>[71](#o71)</sup>.
- **Children under 18** on the day you apply, unmarried, including your wife's children and children born outside marriage; the other parent, if alive, must consent <sup>[71](#o71)</sup>.
- **Adult children** only if they depend on you because of a health condition amounting to total disability <sup>[71](#o71)</sup>. A doctor appointed by the Italian Embassy certifies the condition, at your cost; the same applies to the illness of the other children when a parent over 65 comes for that reason <sup>[134 (2024)](#o134)</sup>.
- **Parents** who depend on you and have no other children in Bangladesh, or parents over 65 whose other children cannot support them for documented serious health reasons <sup>[71](#o71), [72](#o72)</sup>.

The other parent of your child under 18 living legally in Italy can also come, to join the child, if the income and housing rules are met; your income and housing count for this <sup>[134 (2024)](#o134)</sup>. No other relatives, such as brothers, sisters or in-laws, can come on a family visa <sup>[72](#o72)</sup>.

<a id="who-can-sponsor-and-the-income-line"></a>

### 10.2 Who can sponsor, and the income line

1. <a id="who-can-sponsor-and-the-income-line-item-1"></a>**Your permit:** an EU long-term permit, or a permit of at least one year for work, self-employment, international protection, study, religious or family reasons <sup>[184](#o184)</sup>. An EU Blue Card holder can bring family whatever the length of the permit <sup>[66](#o66)</sup>. An asylum seeker still waiting for a final decision cannot apply <sup>[71](#o71)</sup>.
2. <a id="who-can-sponsor-and-the-income-line-item-2"></a>**Two years in Italy:** to bring a wife or husband, a disabled adult child or a parent, you must have lived legally in Italy without a break for at least two years when you apply <sup>[184](#o184)</sup>. This does not apply to children under 18, or to people with international protection <sup>[184](#o184)</sup>.
3. <a id="who-can-sponsor-and-the-income-line-item-3"></a>**Income:** a lawful yearly income of at least the assegno sociale (Italy's state minimum pension, EUR 7,101.12 a year in 2026) plus half of it for each person you bring <sup>[71](#o71), [159](#o159)</sup>. Count it from last year's tax return or CUD (Certificazione Unica, the employer's yearly income statement), or from all your payslips if you started work less than a year ago <sup>[219 (2024)](#o219)</sup>. The income of relatives living with you also counts <sup>[71](#o71)</sup>. Refugees and people with subsidiary protection (the second form of international protection) don't have to meet the income line <sup>[134 (2024)](#o134)</sup>. The 2026 lines are <sup>[159](#o159), [1](#o1)</sup>:
   - 1 person: EUR 10,651.68 a year (about Tk 14.9 lakh) <sup>[159](#o159), [1](#o1)</sup>;
   - 2 people: EUR 14,202.24 (about Tk 19.8 lakh) <sup>[159](#o159), [1](#o1)</sup>;
   - 3 people: EUR 17,752.80 (about Tk 24.8 lakh) <sup>[159](#o159), [1](#o1)</sup>;
   - each more person: EUR 3,550.56 (about Tk 5 lakh) more <sup>[159](#o159), [1](#o1)</sup>;
   - two or more children under 14: at least EUR 14,202.24 in any case <sup>[71](#o71), [159](#o159)</sup>.
4. <a id="who-can-sponsor-and-the-income-line-item-4"></a>**Housing:** a home that meets health and hygiene standards, certified suitable by the comune (municipality) (see [Family reunification](#family-reunification), [Step 1: Get the housing suitability certificate](#step-1-get-the-housing-suitability-certificate)); for a single child under 14, the consent of the home's holder is enough <sup>[71](#o71)</sup>.
5. <a id="who-can-sponsor-and-the-income-line-item-5"></a>**A parent over 65:** you also need health insurance for them covering all risks in Italy, or their enrolment in the SSN (Servizio Sanitario Nazionale, the national health service) for a fee <sup>[71](#o71)</sup>.

**Coming together:** a worker who holds an entry visa for a job contract of at least one year can bring the family at the same time, under the same housing and income rules (familiari al seguito) <sup>[71](#o71)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Does the SUI count the two years from the date on the first permit or from the date of entry on the visa?
> - What income does the SUI accept for a worker whose contract is under a year: the payslips so far multiplied out, or only last year's tax return?
> - What is the SSN fee for enrolling a parent over 65 in 2026, and which insurers do Bangladeshi families use instead?

<a id="what-it-costs"></a>

### 10.3 What it costs

- **Revenue stamps (marca da bollo):** EUR 16 (about Tk 2,200) entered in the online application, and a second EUR 16 stamp for the nulla osta <sup>[N59](#n59), [1](#o1)</sup>.
- **Visa fee in Dhaka:** Tk 16,240 (EUR 116) <sup>[2](#o2)</sup>.
- **VFS service charge:** Tk 5,320 (EUR 38), VAT included, plus a Tk 270 bank draft charge <sup>[2](#o2)</sup>.

VFS takes cash only, at the Embassy's rate of EUR 1 = Tk 140 <sup>[2](#o2)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - What does a comune charge for the housing suitability certificate (Rome, Milan, Venice), and what do the geometri (surveyors) who draw the floor plan charge?
> - What does MoFA charge to apostille a nikahnama, a family certificate and a birth certificate in 2026?
> - What do patronati and CAF (tax-help centres) in Rome and Milan charge to file a family reunification application, and what do Bangladeshi "agents" charge for the whole job?

<a id="step-1-get-the-housing-suitability-certificate"></a>

### 10.4 Step 1: Get the housing suitability certificate

The certificato di idoneità alloggiativa (housing suitability certificate) is issued by the comune where you live <sup>[219 (2024)](#o219), [71](#o71)</sup>. It is valid for at most 6 months from issue <sup>[134 (2024)](#o134)</sup>.

You'll need:

- a rental contract, registered and running at least six months from the date you will apply <sup>[219 (2024)](#o219)</sup>, or, if you own the home, the purchase deed (contratto di compravendita) <sup>[219 (2024)](#o219)</sup>, or a registered comodato d'uso (free-loan contract) running at least six months, with form S2 signed by the owner <sup>[134 (2024)](#o134)</sup>; if you or your family will live as guests in someone else's home, the host's rental contract, deed or loan contract, the host's family status certificate, a declaration of who else lives there, and form S2 signed by the owner and by the tenant if different <sup>[134 (2024)](#o134)</sup>;
- if you are not the only holder of the home, form S2 signed by the holder(s), consenting to host your family, with a signed copy of their ID <sup>[219 (2024)](#o219)</sup>.

**In Rome:**

1. Fill in the request online on the Prefettura di Roma's RIA site, [servizi.utgroma.it/ria/richiesta/nuova](http://servizi.utgroma.it/ria/richiesta/nuova), and give your email address: you are told by email when the Municipio has told the Prefettura it has your documents, and the outcome <sup>[220](#o220)</sup>.
2. Print the request with the list of attached documents and hand it in at your Municipio <sup>[220](#o220)</sup>.
3. Keep the receipt with the RIA code: you can attach it to the nulla osta application if the certificate has not arrived yet <sup>[219 (2024)](#o219)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - How do Milan and other comuni take the request, how long do they and Rome's Municipi take in 2026, and do they send an inspector?
> - What floor area per person do comuni apply, and is a flat shared by several Bangladeshi families ever accepted?

<a id="step-2-apply-for-the-nulla-osta-on-the-ali-portal"></a>

### 10.5 Step 2: Apply for the nulla osta on the ALI portal

You apply online, on the Interior Ministry's immigration portal (ALI); the law gives the SUI 150 days to decide <sup>[221 (2023)](#o221), [71](#o71), [49](#o49)</sup>. Employers' associations, trade unions and authorised associations can file the application for you <sup>[134 (2024)](#o134)</sup>.

You'll need:

- SPID (Sistema Pubblico di Identità Digitale, Italy's free digital ID), opened with an email, a mobile number, a valid ID and your health card with your codice fiscale (tax code) <sup>[N59](#n59)</sup>;
- a EUR 16 revenue stamp <sup>[N59](#n59)</sup>;
- scans of your passport and your family's passports (the pages with number and personal details), your valid permit (or the expired one with the renewal receipt), your codice fiscale and your family status certificate from the comune <sup>[219 (2024)](#o219)</sup>;
- the housing certificate ([Family reunification](#family-reunification), [Step 1: Get the housing suitability certificate](#step-1-get-the-housing-suitability-certificate)), or the receipt of its request <sup>[219 (2024)](#o219)</sup>;
- income papers: last tax return or CUD (all payslips if employed under a year), your contract or Unilav (the employer's hiring notice), the last three payslips, and form S3 signed by your employer within the last month showing the job continues and your monthly pay <sup>[219 (2024)](#o219)</sup>; a domestic worker gives instead the last tax return (730 or Unico) with its e-filing receipt, the hiring notice filed with INPS (Istituto Nazionale della Previdenza Sociale, Italy's social security agency), the last three INPS contribution slips with proof of payment, and form S3 <sup>[134 (2024)](#o134)</sup>; a self-employed worker gives a visura camerale (chamber of commerce extract) no more than a month old, any licence or professional registration the work needs, and an accountant's statement (bilancino) of this year's income, plus last year's Modello Unico tax return if the business is over a year old <sup>[134 (2024)](#o134)</sup>;
- for a parent over 65, your signed undertaking to take out health insurance <sup>[219 (2024)](#o219)</sup>.

1. <a id="step-2-apply-for-the-nulla-osta-on-the-ali-portal-item-1"></a>**Log in with SPID** at nullaostalavoro.dlci.interno.it, fill in the family reunification form, and enter the revenue stamp's number <sup>[221 (2023)](#o221), [N59](#n59)</sup>.
2. <a id="step-2-apply-for-the-nulla-osta-on-the-ali-portal-item-2"></a>**Attach the documents as PDF, JPEG or TIFF files of at most 3MB each,** with forms S1 (one child under 14), S2 and S3 or S3 bis as needed <sup>[221 (2023)](#o221), [134 (2024)](#o134)</sup>.
3. <a id="step-2-apply-for-the-nulla-osta-on-the-ali-portal-item-3"></a>**Watch the "communications" section** of the ALI portal and your email: the SUI first asks the Questura (provincial police headquarters) to check for criminal records, then handles files in order of arrival <sup>[221 (2023)](#o221)</sup>.
4. <a id="step-2-apply-for-the-nulla-osta-on-the-ali-portal-item-4"></a>**If you get a notice that papers are missing,** go to the SUI on the date it gives with the missing papers and the originals; if they are still incomplete that day, the application is rejected <sup>[221 (2023)](#o221)</sup>.
5. <a id="step-2-apply-for-the-nulla-osta-on-the-ali-portal-item-5"></a>**Go to the SUI when called** with the original revenue stamp, a second EUR 16 stamp, your permit and a copy <sup>[N59](#n59)</sup>.
6. <a id="step-2-apply-for-the-nulla-osta-on-the-ali-portal-item-6"></a>**When the nulla osta is issued,** the SUI sends it electronically to the Embassy in Dhaka; send the original to your family <sup>[221 (2023)](#o221), [N59](#n59)</sup>.

**Check it is real:** a genuine nulla osta appears in your own ALI portal account, under communications <sup>[221 (2023)](#o221)</sup>. Check that your family's names, sex and dates of birth on it match their passports exactly, or the visa will be badly delayed <sup>[222](#o222), [223 (2024)](#o223)</sup>.

**Time limit:** the nulla osta is valid 6 months from the date it is issued and cannot be extended; your family must apply for the visa within that time <sup>[221 (2023)](#o221), [134 (2024)](#o134)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - How long do the SUIs in Rome, Milan and Venice actually take to issue a family nulla osta in 2026?


<a id="step-3-prepare-and-apostille-the-papers-in-bangladesh"></a>

### 10.6 Step 3: Prepare and apostille the papers in Bangladesh

Bangladesh joined the Apostille Convention on 30 March 2025, and the Embassy now wants the marriage, family and child documents in original with an apostille from MoFA (Bangladesh's Ministry of Foreign Affairs), one apostille per document <sup>[224](#o224), [101](#o101), [72](#o72)</sup>. How to get an apostille: see ["Work visa: Decreto Flussi worker (non-seasonal)"](#work-visa-decreto-flussi-worker-non-seasonal), [Step 4: Passport, police clearance and certificates](#step-4-passport-police-clearance-and-certificates) <sup>[86](#o86)</sup>.

You'll need:

- **For a wife or husband:** the nikahnama (Muslim marriage contract) in Bangla and English and the marriage certificate, both original and apostilled; the marriage certificate must give the solemnisation date (nikahnama column 12), the registration date (column 24), and the volume, page and serial number; if either of you married before, the apostilled talaknama (divorce deed); Hindu and other-faith couples bring the marriage certificate or a true copy of the register, translated into English and apostilled <sup>[72](#o72), [105](#o105)</sup>;
- **For every applicant:** a family certificate (paribarik sanad) from the Union Parishad (rural council) chairman or the ward councillor, original and apostilled <sup>[185](#o185), [72](#o72)</sup>;
- **For children:** the original birth certificate and both parents' consent, apostilled; for a guardian, court papers from a Bangladeshi court, apostilled <sup>[72](#o72), [105](#o105)</sup>;
- **For parents:** a dependent certificate from the chairman or councillor on letterhead, or from a lawyer on stamp paper <sup>[185](#o185)</sup>.

1. <a id="step-3-prepare-and-apostille-the-papers-in-bangladesh-item-1"></a>**Make every name match.** Name, father's name and date of birth must be identical in every document, with no exceptions; your wife's passport must show your name as her husband <sup>[185](#o185)</sup>.
2. <a id="step-3-prepare-and-apostille-the-papers-in-bangladesh-item-2"></a>**Get the family certificate in the Embassy's format:** issued in the name of the living head of the family, listing all immediate family members from senior to junior, with dead members marked "late" and real addresses (your Italian address if you live in Italy) <sup>[185](#o185)</sup>.
3. <a id="step-3-prepare-and-apostille-the-papers-in-bangladesh-item-3"></a>**If you married by phone or video,** make sure the nikahnama says so and shows the kazi (marriage registrar) was physically present <sup>[185](#o185)</sup>.
4. <a id="step-3-prepare-and-apostille-the-papers-in-bangladesh-item-4"></a>**Do not use correction fluid or overwrite anything** <sup>[185](#o185)</sup>.

**Check it is real:** the Embassy verifies every civil status document, and the law lets it hold the visa until it has <sup>[71](#o71)</sup>. If it doubts the papers, it can confirm a parent or child relationship by a DNA test the family pays for <sup>[71](#o71)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Does the Embassy in Dhaka ask Bangladeshi families for DNA tests in practice, which laboratory does it use, and what does the test cost?

<a id="step-4-the-visa-at-vfs-dhaka"></a>

### 10.7 Step 4: The visa at VFS Dhaka

You'll need:

- the D-type (long-stay) visa form, signed (both parents sign for a child) <sup>[72](#o72), [223 (2024)](#o223)</sup>;
- a recent ICAO photo (the international passport-photo standard): 3.5 x 4.0 cm, in colour, white background, taken within the last 6 months <sup>[72](#o72)</sup>;
- a passport valid at least three months beyond the visa, with two blank pages <sup>[72](#o72)</sup>;
- a copy of the nulla osta <sup>[72](#o72)</sup>;
- a one-way flight booking with the booking reference <sup>[72](#o72)</sup>;
- copies of all the sponsor's passports, past and present, all pages, and the sponsor's Italian permit and identity card <sup>[72](#o72), [185](#o185)</sup>;
- the documents from Family reunification, [Step 3: Prepare and apostille the papers in Bangladesh](#step-3-prepare-and-apostille-the-papers-in-bangladesh);
- recommended for a wife: certified copies of all pages of the husband's passports since the wedding and the ID of all wedding witnesses, and for a phone or video marriage the ID and Italian permits of the witnesses in Italy <sup>[72](#o72)</sup>;
- the fees (see [What it costs](#what-it-costs)).

1. <a id="step-4-the-visa-at-vfs-dhaka-item-1"></a>**Book and submit at a VFS centre** with all originals (addresses and booking in Visit visa (Schengen), [Step 2: Apply for the nulla osta on the ALI portal](#step-2-apply-for-the-nulla-osta-on-the-ali-portal)) <sup>[185](#o185), [216](#o216)</sup>.
2. <a id="step-4-the-visa-at-vfs-dhaka-item-2"></a>**Give fingerprints,** except children aged 12 or under <sup>[195](#o195), [2](#o2)</sup>.
3. <a id="step-4-the-visa-at-vfs-dhaka-item-3"></a>**For a wife, bring passport stamps showing the husband was in Bangladesh for the wedding** <sup>[185](#o185)</sup>.
4. <a id="step-4-the-visa-at-vfs-dhaka-item-4"></a>**File only one application per person:** the same person cannot have two applications at once, even with two passports <sup>[72](#o72)</sup>.
5. <a id="step-4-the-visa-at-vfs-dhaka-item-5"></a>**Track the application on VFS's passport tracker** and keep the phone number and email you gave working: if the Embassy wants more documents, the tracker shows it with a deadline, and missed messages are your own responsibility <sup>[112](#o112)</sup>.
6. <a id="step-4-the-visa-at-vfs-dhaka-item-6"></a>**Collect the passport within 90 days,** or it goes back to the Embassy and on to Bangladesh's Ministry of Home Affairs <sup>[112](#o112)</sup>.

A family reunion visa needs a minimum of 21 calendar days at VFS <sup>[185](#o185)</sup>; by law the Embassy issues it within 30 days of the application, unless checks with foreign authorities are needed <sup>[113](#o113)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - How long did a family visa take at VFS Dhaka in 2026, from submission to passport return?
> - Does VFS turn away family applicants whose passport has less than 1 year and 6 months' validity, as its own information page asks?
> - Does BMET (the Bureau of Manpower, Employment and Training) or Dhaka airport immigration ask a wife on a family visa for clearance or any other paper?

<a id="step-5-after-arrival"></a>

### 10.8 Step 5: After arrival

1. <a id="step-5-after-arrival-item-1"></a>**Within 48 hours,** you or the host give a written declaration of hospitality to the police or the comune <sup>[221 (2023)](#o221)</sup>.
2. <a id="step-5-after-arrival-item-2"></a>**Within 8 working days,** book an SUI appointment on the ALI portal ("book an appointment") and go to it, with the SPID used for the application; the passport entry stamp proves the date <sup>[221 (2023)](#o221), [134 (2024)](#o134)</sup>. For a parent over 65, take out the health insurance in this time, before going to the SUI <sup>[134 (2024)](#o134)</sup>.
3. <a id="step-5-after-arrival-item-3"></a>**At the SUI,** the entry is registered, the residence permit papers are completed and the family member gets a codice fiscale; the permit is the permesso di soggiorno per motivi familiari (family residence permit) <sup>[221 (2023)](#o221), [122 (2023)](#o122), [186](#o186)</sup>. Each family member over 16 also signs the integration agreement and is called to a civic session (see ["Work visa: Decreto Flussi worker (non-seasonal)"](#work-visa-decreto-flussi-worker-non-seasonal), [Step 7, item 4: Sign the Accordo di integrazione (integration agreement)](#step-7-after-arrival-item-4)) <sup>[134 (2024)](#o134), [125](#o125)</sup>.
4. <a id="step-5-after-arrival-item-4"></a>**Register the family's residence at the comune within 20 days of posting the permit application,** with copies of the nulla osta, the permit application form (Mod. 209) and the post-office receipt <sup>[134 (2024)](#o134)</sup>. For health service enrolment and fingerprints, follow "Work visa: Decreto Flussi worker (non-seasonal)", [Step 7: After arrival](#step-7-after-arrival); health cover extends to dependent family members staying legally in Italy <sup>[139](#o139)</sup>.

<a id="keeping-the-family-permit"></a>

### 10.9 Keeping the family permit

- It lasts as long as the sponsor's permit and is renewed with it <sup>[186](#o186)</sup>.
- It allows work as an employee or self-employed, study, and registering at job centres <sup>[186](#o186)</sup>.
- A child under 18 living with you gets a family permit valid until 18, or the EU long-term permit if you hold one <sup>[160](#o160)</sup>.
- If the sponsor dies, or the couple legally separate or divorce, the family permit can be converted into a work or study permit <sup>[186](#o186)</sup>. It can also be converted into a work permit on request whenever you meet the conditions for one <sup>[134 (2024)](#o134)</sup>.
- A permit given on marriage is revoked at once if the couple do not actually live together, unless they have a child <sup>[186](#o186)</sup>. A marriage made only to get a permit gets the application refused <sup>[71](#o71)</sup>.

<a id="if-the-nulla-osta-or-visa-is-refused"></a>

### 10.10 If the nulla osta or visa is refused

You can challenge a refused nulla osta or family permit in the ordinary court (tribunale ordinario) of the place where you live <sup>[186](#o186), [134 (2024)](#o134)</sup>. A refused family visa can also be appealed to the ordinary court, with no time limit, and the appeal is served on the Avvocatura dello Stato (the State Legal Service) <sup>[115](#o115)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - What is the deadline to appeal a refused family nulla osta, and which lawyers or patronati (free welfare advice offices, most run by trade unions) in Rome and Milan take these cases?

<a id="scams-3"></a>

### 10.11 Scams

- **Fake civil status papers:** in May 2025 the Embassy said it was finding more and more fake Bangladeshi and Italian civil status documents in family applications <sup>[225](#o225)</sup>. A forged paper means refusal and a flag in the Schengen system that permanently affects future travel to the EU <sup>[225](#o225)</sup>. Each applicant is personally responsible for every document filed in their name <sup>[105](#o105)</sup>. A sponsor who makes false statements or files forged papers is reported to the courts in Italy and in his home country <sup>[134 (2024)](#o134)</sup>.
- **Duplicate applications:** a second application in another passport is not allowed <sup>[72](#o72), [105](#o105)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Do brokers in Rome or Milan sell fake employer S3 statements, payslips or rental contracts to reach the income and housing line, and what do they charge?
> - Do brokers in Dhaka sell "fast-track" family visa appointments at VFS?

<a id="family-of-an-italian-or-eu-citizen"></a>

### 10.12 Family of an Italian or EU citizen

- **Visa:** the wife or husband, parents, parents-in-law and children under 21 of an Italian or EU citizen apply at VFS for the EU family member visa <sup>[185](#o185)</sup>. The visa fee and VFS charge are waived for those who prove they depend on the citizen <sup>[185](#o185)</sup>. They need an invitation letter from the citizen stating the intention to exercise the right to family unity, with the citizen's ID; a wife or husband also needs the marriage extract from the Italian comune plus the apostilled nikahnama and marriage certificate <sup>[226](#o226)</sup>.
- **Residence card:** after three months the family member applies to the Questura for the residence card of a family member of an EU citizen, valid five years and free apart from the cost of the forms <sup>[227](#o227)</sup>.
- **Marrying in Italy:** a foreigner legally resident for at least a year who marries an Italian, an EU citizen or a legal resident of Italy can get a family permit <sup>[186](#o186)</sup>.
- **Parent of an Italian child:** a foreign parent of an Italian child living in Italy gets a family permit even without a valid permit <sup>[186](#o186)</sup>.
- **Citizenship by marriage:** the spouse of an Italian can apply after two years' legal residence in Italy after the marriage (three years if living abroad), halved if the couple have children <sup>[228](#o228)</sup>.

<a id="changing-visa-type"></a>

## 11 Changing visa type

- **Visit visa to anything else:** Not allowed. A tourist visa does not allow work, study or applying to stay <sup>[31](#o31)</sup>.
- **Study permit to work permit:** Allowed, outside the Decreto Flussi quotas, before the study permit expires, once a contratto di soggiorno (contract of stay) is signed at the SUI or, for self-employment, once the certification that the self-employment requirements are met is issued <sup>[229](#o229), [124 (2024)](#o124)</sup>. People admitted for a training course or traineeship can convert only after finishing it <sup>[205](#o205)</sup>. A student who wants to work more than 20 hours a week must convert first <sup>[205](#o205), [206 (2022)](#o206)</sup>.
- **Study to job search, after graduating:** Allowed. A graduate with a degree, master's, PhD or ITS (Istituto Tecnologico Superiore, higher technical) diploma from Italy can get a permit of 9 to 12 months to look for a job or start a business linked to the studies, then convert it to a work permit <sup>[204](#o204)</sup>. This needs the minimum income set for family reunification, health cover, and registering as available for work at a Centro per l'Impiego (public job centre) <sup>[204](#o204)</sup>.
- **Seasonal to non-seasonal work:** Allowed after at least three months of regular work, if an employer offers a fixed-term or permanent contract; ask the SUI (see [Work visa: seasonal worker](#work-visa-seasonal-worker)) <sup>[190](#o190)</sup>.
- **Family permit to work or study:** Not needed to work, since the family permit already allows it <sup>[186](#o186)</sup>. If the sponsor dies, or the couple separate or divorce, it can be converted into a work or study permit <sup>[186](#o186)</sup>.
- **Any permit to family permit:** A foreigner legally resident at least a year who marries in Italy an Italian, an EU citizen or a legal resident can get a family permit <sup>[186](#o186)</sup>. A family member already living legally in Italy can convert their own permit into a family permit within a year of its expiry, if the sponsor meets the requirements <sup>[186](#o186)</sup>.
- **Asylum seeker's or special protection permit to work permit:** Not allowed. An asylum seeker's permit cannot be converted into a work permit even after working, and special protection permits cannot be converted since 2023 (see [Asylum](#asylum)) <sup>[N60](#n60), [N39](#n39)</sup>.

A permit must be issued, renewed or converted within 90 days of the application <sup>[9](#o9)</sup>. While waiting with the post office receipt, you may stay and work until the police notify a reason to refuse <sup>[49](#o49)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - How long does a study-to-work conversion take at the SUI in Rome or Milan in 2026, and do Questure still apply the old rule (DPR 394/1999, art. 14) that counts it within the quotas?

<a id="having-a-child-in-italy"></a>

## 12 Having a child in Italy

A child born in Italy to Bangladeshi parents is not Italian at birth, because Italian citizenship passes by blood <sup>[163](#o163)</sup>. The child follows the legal status of the parent it lives with, and needs an Italian permit, a Bangladeshi birth registration and a Bangladeshi passport <sup>[160](#o160), [230](#o230)</sup>.

<a id="step-1-register-the-birth-in-italy"></a>

### 12.1 Step 1: Register the birth in Italy

1. <a id="step-1-register-the-birth-in-italy-item-1"></a>**Declare the birth** within 3 days at the hospital's office (direzione sanitaria), or within 10 days at the comune where the baby was born or at your own comune of residence <sup>[231](#o231)</sup>.
2. <a id="step-1-register-the-birth-in-italy-item-2"></a>**Get the atto di nascita (Italian birth record)** naming the child and both parents; the Bangladesh Embassy needs it for the birth registration and the child's passport <sup>[230](#o230)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - How long does the comune take to record the birth so the atto di nascita can be printed, and does parents' missing residence registration delay it?

<a id="step-2-get-the-childs-residence-permit"></a>

### 12.2 Step 2: Get the child's residence permit

A child under 18 living with a legally resident parent gets a family permit valid until 18, or the EU long-term permit if the parent holds one <sup>[160](#o160)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Is a newborn's permit requested with the post office kit or directly at the Questura, and what does it cost?

<a id="step-3-register-the-birth-with-the-bangladesh-embassy-in-rome"></a>

### 12.3 Step 3: Register the birth with the Bangladesh Embassy in Rome

1. <a id="step-3-register-the-birth-with-the-bangladesh-embassy-in-rome-item-1"></a>**Apply online at [bdris.gov.bd](https://bdris.gov.bd/),** Bangladesh's birth and death registration system, choosing the Embassy in Rome as the office; an application not submitted within 15 days is deleted <sup>[230](#o230)</sup>.
2. <a id="step-3-register-the-birth-with-the-bangladesh-embassy-in-rome-item-2"></a>**Submit the printed application at the Embassy** with the Italian birth record naming the child and both parents and copies of both parents' passports; the baby does not need to come <sup>[230](#o230)</sup>. The fee is EUR 1 (about Tk 140) <sup>[232](#o232), [1](#o1)</sup>.
3. <a id="step-3-register-the-birth-with-the-bangladesh-embassy-in-rome-item-3"></a>**Collect the certificate:** it usually takes 5 working days <sup>[230](#o230)</sup>.

Register a birth only once: registering the same person twice is an offence <sup>[230](#o230)</sup>. The Consulate in Milan also registers births, with the online bdris form, both parents' passport copies, the comune or hospital birth certificate naming the child and both parents, and one colour photo; a parent must come in person <sup>[233](#o233)</sup>. To correct a registration later, fill in the form at [bdris.gov.bd/br/correction](https://bdris.gov.bd/br/correction) and attach the proof online, such as the atto di nascita for a wrong name or age <sup>[233](#o233)</sup>.

<a id="step-4-get-the-child-a-bangladeshi-passport"></a>

### 12.4 Step 4: Get the child a Bangladeshi passport

1. <a id="step-4-get-the-child-a-bangladeshi-passport-item-1"></a>**Fill in the e-passport form at [epassport.gov.bd/onboarding](https://www.epassport.gov.bd/onboarding)** and print it with its barcode (see [Help from the Bangladesh Embassy in Rome and Consulate in Milan](#help-from-the-bangladesh-embassy-in-rome-and-consulate-in-milan), item 1); machine-readable passports stopped on 31 December 2025 <sup>[233](#o233)</sup>.
2. <a id="step-4-get-the-child-a-bangladeshi-passport-item-2"></a>**Book one appointment for the whole family** online at [bdembassyrome.com](https://www.bdembassyrome.com) <sup>[230](#o230), [234](#o234)</sup>. In Milan a newborn's first passport needs no appointment <sup>[233](#o233)</sup>.
3. <a id="step-4-get-the-child-a-bangladeshi-passport-item-3"></a>**Bring** the printed form, the Bangladeshi birth registration certificate, a copy of the Italian birth record, copies of both parents' passports, and a 4R-size photo of the child <sup>[230](#o230), [233](#o233)</sup>. For a child under 6 the photo must be clear, on a white background, face straight, both ears showing, with no cap or band, or the application is refused; from age 6 the photo is taken by camera at enrolment <sup>[233](#o233)</sup>. Under the old machine-readable passport rules a child under 2 did not need to come; an older child had to <sup>[230](#o230)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - For a child's first e-passport, must a child under 2 come to the Embassy or Consulate, now that under-6s give no fingerprints and their photo is uploaded?
4. <a id="step-4-get-the-child-a-bangladeshi-passport-item-4"></a>**Pay by bank card,** not cash: a child's passport costs EUR 100 (about Tk 14,000) <sup>[230](#o230), [1](#o1)</sup>.

The passport is printed in Dhaka and usually reaches the Embassy within 2 months; there is no urgent service <sup>[230](#o230)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Does the Rome Embassy charge EUR 100 for a child's first passport, as its FAQ says, or EUR 105 or 130 as on its 2026 fee list, and is it still paid at the counter (the old form's "Skip Payment") with the e-passport form?

<a id="can-the-child-become-italian"></a>

### 12.5 Can the child become Italian?

- **At 18:** a child born in Italy who has lived there legally without a break becomes Italian by declaring it within one year of turning 18 <sup>[167](#o167)</sup>.
- **Earlier:** a child born in Italy can apply after three years' legal residence <sup>[162 (2024)](#o162)</sup>.

For the parents' own citizenship, see [Becoming an Italian citizen](#becoming-an-italian-citizen).

**Travelling to Bangladesh on an Italian passport:** the child needs a No Visa Required (NVR) seal from the Bangladesh Embassy <sup>[235](#o235)</sup>:

1. Fill in the form on visa.gov.bd <sup>[235](#o235)</sup>.
2. Email it with the passports to bdvisa.rome@gmail.com and wait for approval <sup>[235](#o235)</sup>.
3. Book an appointment and pay EUR 50 (about Tk 7,000) per passport; the seal takes 5 working days <sup>[235](#o235), [1](#o1)</sup>.

A child without a Bangladeshi passport also needs the Bangladeshi birth certificate and the parents' Bangladeshi passports for the seal <sup>[235](#o235)</sup>. In Milan, hand in the online visa form with the photo uploaded, one passport-size colour photo, and copies of the Bangladeshi passport, the parents' passports and the birth registration or NID; from outside Milan add a DHL or TNT return envelope <sup>[233](#o233)</sup>.

<a id="if-a-parent-has-no-permit"></a>

### 12.6 If a parent has no permit

The juvenile court can authorise a parent or other family member to enter or stay in Italy for a set time, for serious reasons linked to a child living in Italy <sup>[160](#o160)</sup>. A foreign parent of an Italian child living in Italy gets a family permit even without a valid permit <sup>[186](#o186)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - How does an undocumented Bangladeshi parent apply to the juvenile court for this authorisation, and which patronati or lawyers in Rome and Milan handle it?

<a id="asylum"></a>

## 13 Asylum

Asylum (international protection) is only for someone who cannot go home because they fear persecution or serious harm and cannot get protection from their own country <sup>[N61](#n61)</sup>. Italy and the EU both list Bangladesh as a "safe country of origin" (a country whose people are presumed not to need protection), and the EU list applies from 12 June 2026 <sup>[19](#o19), [20](#o20)</sup>. In 2025 Italy refused 26,240 of 29,595 Bangladeshi claims at first decision, 88.66% <sup>[N7](#n7)</sup>. Across all nationalities Italy's first-instance protection rate that year was 30%, and four of the five main nationalities of applicants (Bangladesh, Peru, Pakistan, Egypt and Morocco) are on Italy's safe list <sup>[N7](#n7), [N62](#n62)</sup>. From January to September 2026 it refused 24,065 of 27,790 and gave refugee status to 375 people <sup>[236](#o236)</sup>. A refusal is also a return order and an entry ban <sup>[N39](#n39)</sup>. Italy's Ambassador in Dhaka said in November 2025 that most Bangladeshis who arrive irregularly ask for asylum with false papers, fail and have to go home <sup>[N63](#n63)</sup>.

Bangladeshis were still the largest group of applicants in Italy in 2025: 28,365 people, with 37,130 cases waiting at year end <sup>[N7](#n7)</sup>. First-time applications from Bangladeshis fell from 1,875 in June 2026 to 720 in July, the first full month of the new EU rules <sup>[237](#o237), [20](#o20)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - How many Bangladeshis who entered on a Decreto Flussi (Italy's yearly work-quota decree) visa later applied for asylum in 2025-2026, and how did their cases end?

<a id="bangladesh-is-a-safe-country"></a>

### 13.1 Bangladesh is a "safe country"

Bangladesh has been on Italy's list of safe countries of origin since a Ministerial Decree of 7 May 2024 <sup>[N8](#n8)</sup>. In October 2024 the government wrote the list into Article 2-bis of Legislative Decree 25/2008 (the Procedure Decree, Italy's main asylum procedure law), and Bangladesh is still on it <sup>[N8](#n8), [19](#o19)</sup>. On 23 February 2026 the EU adopted its first common list, Regulation (EU) 2026/464: Bangladesh, Colombia, Egypt, India, Kosovo, Morocco and Tunisia <sup>[20](#o20), [238](#o238)</sup>. In listing Bangladesh, the EU noted that six member states already treated it as safe and found, in general, no persecution there <sup>[238](#o238)</sup>. It is part of the EU Asylum Procedure Regulation and applies from 12 June 2026 with the rest of the EU Pact on Migration and Asylum (the EU's common asylum and return rules), and Italy keeps its own list alongside it <sup>[238](#o238), [20](#o20)</sup>. The European Commission can suspend a country from the EU list after an assessment <sup>[239](#o239)</sup>.

What it means for an applicant:

- **You must prove Bangladesh is unsafe for you personally.** A claim can be rejected as "manifestly unfounded" (obviously without basis) if you have not shown serious reasons why Bangladesh is not safe for you in particular <sup>[N8](#n8)</sup>. Each claim is still assessed individually, and the EU rules name LGBTIQ people, victims of gender-based violence, human rights defenders, religious minorities and journalists as needing special attention <sup>[238](#o238)</sup>. The Questura (the provincial police headquarters, which registers asylum claims) must warn you of this when the claim is lodged <sup>[N8](#n8)</sup>.
- **Fast track.** A nationality with an EU-wide recognition rate of 20% or lower goes into the accelerated procedure, which must end within three months <sup>[240 (2024)](#o240)</sup>. Across the EU, 4% of Bangladeshi applicants succeeded in 2024 <sup>[238](#o238)</sup>.
- **Border procedure.** Italy's law implementing the Pact makes the border procedure compulsory for people from countries with an acceptance rate below 20% <sup>[N64](#n64)</sup>. Italy must examine up to 16,032 claims a year this way between 12 June 2026 and 12 June 2027 <sup>[N64](#n64)</sup>. During it you must stay at or near the border or in a designated place, which does not count as entering Italy <sup>[N64](#n64)</sup>; in practice this has amounted to detention <sup>[N65](#n65)</sup>. The Territorial Commission (the local panel that decides asylum claims) must decide within four weeks, and the whole border procedure must end within twelve weeks <sup>[N66](#n66), [241](#o241), [242](#o242)</sup>.

**The August 2025 court ruling:** on 1 August 2025 the EU Court of Justice ruled, in the case of two Bangladeshis taken from the sea to Italy's detention centre in Albania, that a country designated safe by law must be open to review by a court, on sources the applicant can see <sup>[243](#o243)</sup>. It also ruled that a country that does not protect its whole population cannot be listed, but only until the new EU Regulation took effect on 12 June 2026, which allows exceptions for groups of people <sup>[243](#o243)</sup>. The ruling affected transfers to Albania but did not take Bangladesh off either list <sup>[N65](#n65), [19](#o19), [20](#o20)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Has any Italian court since 12 June 2026 refused to apply the safe-country presumption to a Bangladeshi applicant (for example an Awami League activist after 2024), and on what grounds?
> - Has Italy named any group of Bangladeshis excluded from the safe-country designation?

<a id="how-to-apply"></a>

### 13.2 How to apply

These rules apply to claims made from 12 June 2026; a claim made before that date continues under the old rules until it ends <sup>[241](#o241)</sup>.

1. <a id="how-to-apply-item-1"></a>**Ask for asylum at the Questura or the border.** Already in Italy, go to the Ufficio immigrazione (Immigration Office) of the nearest Questura; on arrival, ask the Border Police <sup>[N61](#n61)</sup>. You can ask orally or in writing, with a free interpreter <sup>[N61](#n61), [244](#o244)</sup>. People in prison or in a CPR (Centro di Permanenza per i Rimpatri, a closed centre where people are held before removal) can also apply <sup>[N61](#n61), [245](#o245)</sup>. Questure and border police offices registered all asylum claims until 31 October 2026 <sup>[241](#o241)</sup>.

   You'll need <sup>[N61](#n61), [244](#o244)</sup>:
   - your passport, if you have one (the police may ask for it);
   - every document that supports your story;
   - if you want, a written account of your story, in Italian or another language.

   1. Give your personal details and reasons; the police take your photo and fingerprints, which go into Eurodac, the EU fingerprint database <sup>[N61](#n61)</sup>.
   2. Your claim is recorded on form C3 (personal history, journey, reasons for leaving), which you sign; you get a copy <sup>[N67](#n67)</sup>.
   3. You receive a document with your name, a unique identity code, your photo and the details you declared; it proves the claim is registered <sup>[241](#o241)</sup>.
   4. You are given the National Commission for the Right of Asylum's "Practical guide for applicants for international protection in Italy", which exists in Bengali <sup>[244](#o244)</sup>.
   5. If you need a place to live, say so now: a reception place must be asked for when you apply ([While you wait: reception](#while-you-wait-reception-work-and-papers), work and papers, item 1) <sup>[N68](#n68)</sup>.
